        public struct H1CCD
        {
            public double x;
            public double y;

            public double y_Hi;
            public double u_Low;

        public void GetH1CCD()
        {
            H1CCDData.Clear();
            DataTable H1dt = RecipeData.Tables["tb_H1_ScrewCheck"];
            for (int i = 0; i < H1dt.Rows.Count; i++)
            {
                DataRow dr = H1dt.Rows[i];
                H1CCD ToCCDData = new H1CCD
                {
                    x = Convert.ToDouble(dr[0]),
                    y = Convert.ToDouble(dr[1]),
                    u = Convert.ToDouble(dr[2]),
                    x_Low = Convert.ToDouble(dr[3]),
                    x_Hi = Convert.ToDouble(dr[4]),
                    y_Low = Convert.ToDouble(dr[5]),
                    y_Hi = Convert.ToDouble(dr[6]),
                    u_Low = Convert.ToDouble(dr[7]),
                    u_Hi = Convert.ToDouble(dr[8])
                };
                H1CCDData.Add(ToCCDData);
            }
        }

        private void InitBackGround()
        {
            if (bgwEpsonCommunicate == null) { bgwEpsonCommunicate = new BackgroundWorker(); }
            bgwEpsonCommunicate.WorkerSupportsCancellation = true;
            bgwEpsonCommunicate.WorkerReportsProgress = true;
            bgwEpsonCommunicate.DoWork += new DoWorkEventHandler(bgwEpsonCommunicate_DoWork);
            bgwEpsonCommunicate.RunWorkerCompleted += new RunWorkerCompletedEventHandler(bgwEpsonCommunicate_RunWorkerCompleted);
        }


        private void bgwEpsonCommunicate_DoWork(object sender, DoWorkEventArgs e)
        {
            do
            {
                Robot.Recive();
                Thread.Sleep(10);
            } while (Robot.ConnectStatus);
        }

        private void bgwEpsonCommunicate_RunWorkerCompleted(object sender, RunWorkerCompletedEventArgs e)
        {
            Log.log.Write("机器人通讯断开连接", Color.Black);
        }
        public double GetAngle(double[] dOrgP1, double[] dOrgP2, double[] dNewP1, double[] dNewP2)
        {

            Pos4D OrgP1 = new Pos4D();
            OrgP1.x = dOrgP1[0];
            OrgP1.y = dOrgP1[1];

            Pos4D OrgP2 = new Pos4D();
            OrgP2.x = dOrgP2[0];
            OrgP2.y = dOrgP2[1];

            Pos4D NewP1 = new Pos4D();
            NewP1.x = dNewP1[0];
            NewP1.y = dNewP1[1];

            Pos4D NewP2 = new Pos4D();
            NewP2.x = dNewP2[0];
            NewP2.y = dNewP2[1];

            double Angle = GetLineAngle(OrgP1, OrgP2) - GetLineAngle(NewP1, NewP2);
            return Angle;
        }



        private double GetLineAngle(Pos4D P1, Pos4D P2)
        {
            double x = P2.x + P1.x;
            double y = P2.y + P1.y;
            double hypotenuse = Math.Sqrt(Math.Pow(x, 2) + Math.Pow(y, 2));
            double cos = x / hypotenuse;
            double radian = Math.Acos(cos);
            double angle = 180 / (Math.PI / radian);
            if (y < 0)
                angle = -angle;
            else if ((y == 0) && (x < 0))
                angle = 180;
            return angle;
        }

        public Pos4D VisionCalculateOffset(double OreSnapR, double SnapR)
        {
            Pos4D OffsetPos = new Pos4D();

            double OffsetX = 0;
            double OffsetY = 0;
            double OffsetR = 0;
            double Ag1 = GetSettingValue("PSet", "H1-CCD1-U-1") * Math.PI / 180;
            double Ag2 = GetSettingValue("PSet", "H1-Robot-U") * Math.PI / 180;
            double RadiusX = (GetSettingValue("PSet", "H1-CCD1-X-1") - GetSettingValue("PSet", "H1-Robot-X")) / (Math.Cos(Ag1) - Math.Cos(Ag2));
            double RadiusY = (GetSettingValue("PSet", "H1-CCD1-Y-1") - GetSettingValue("PSet", "H1-Robot-Y")) / (Math.Sin(Ag1) - Math.Sin(Ag2));

            double bg1 = OreSnapR * Math.PI / 180;
            double bg2 = SnapR * Math.PI / 180;

            OffsetX = RadiusX * Math.Cos(bg1) - RadiusX * Math.Cos(bg2);
            OffsetY = RadiusY * Math.Sin(bg1) - RadiusY * Math.Sin(bg2);
            OffsetR = OreSnapR - SnapR;

            OffsetPos.x = OffsetX;
            OffsetPos.y = OffsetY;
            OffsetPos.u = OffsetR;
            return OffsetPos;
        }

        public struct Pos4D
        {
            public double x;
            public double y;
            public double z;
            public double u;
        }

        private string sPressureData(string sComment)   // :004RDGROSS=  
        {
            string sData = "";
            string sBuffer = sComment + Environment.NewLine;
            serialPort3.Write(sBuffer);
            Thread.Sleep(25);
            sData = serialPort3.ReadExisting();
            return sData;
        }

        private bool PFKeepAlive()
        {
            string Response = PFClient.SendAndWaitForResponse(MID.M9999, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(2));

            if (Response == null)
            {
                return false;
            }
            if (Response.Contains("00209999"))
            {
                PFClient.CommunicationAlive();
                return true;
            }
            return false;
        }

        private bool PFKeepAlive2()
        {
            string Response = PFClient2.SendAndWaitForResponse(MID.M9999, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(2));

            if (Response == null)
            {
                return false;
            }
            if (Response.Contains("00209999"))
            {
                PFClient2.CommunicationAlive();
                return true;
            }
            return false;
        }

        public void RefreshDifferentThreadUI(Control control, Action action)
        {
            if (control.InvokeRequired)
            {
                Action refreshUI = new Action(action);
                control.Invoke(refreshUI);
            }
            else
            {
                action.Invoke();
            }
        }

        private bool ConnectToRobot()
        {
            try
            {
                if (RobotZebra.Connect(GetRecipeValue("RSet", "RobotPoint1")))
                {
                    return true;
                }
                RobotZebra.Disconnect();
                string RobotScrewIP = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "RobotPoint1");//MSet.RobotIP
                //int iRobotProt = 5000;//GetSettingValue("MSet", "RobotPort");//MSet.RobotPort
                bool bConncet = RobotZebra.Connect(RobotScrewIP);
                DelayMs(100);
                return bConncet;
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                string a = e.ToString();
                return false;
            }

        }

        public static bool DelayMs(int delayMilliseconds)
        {
            DateTime now = DateTime.Now;
            Double s;
            do
            {
                TimeSpan spand = DateTime.Now - now;
                s = spand.TotalMilliseconds + spand.Seconds * 1000;
                Application.DoEvents();
            }
            while (s < delayMilliseconds);
            return true;
        }

        private void Btn_ConnectRobot_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (ConnectToRobot())
            {
                btnConnectResult.Text = "ConnectSuccess";
                btnConnectResult.BackColor = Color.Green;
            }
            else
            {
                btnConnectResult.Text = "ConnectFail";
                btnConnectResult.BackColor = Color.Red;
            }
        }

        public bool ReflashRobotR()
        {
            bool bReturn = false;
            if (ReflashR())
            {
                bReturn = true;
            }
            else
            {
                if (ConnectToRobot())
                {
                    if (ReflashR())
                    {
                        bReturn = true;
                    }
                }
            }
            if (bReturn == false)
            {
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1634");
            }
            return bReturn;
        }

        private static readonly object RobotControl = new object();

        private bool ReflashR()
        {
            lock (RobotControl)
            {
                try
                {
                    return RobotZebra.RefreshR(ref ReadRobotR);
                }
                catch (Exception)
                {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }



        private bool WritePR(int RAddress, double[] PRValue)
        {
            try
            {
                if (ConnectToRobot())
                {
                    RobotZebra.WritePR(RAddress, PRValue);
                }

                return true;
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        private bool WriteToRobotPR(int RAddress, double[] PRValue)
        {
            bool bReturn = false;
            if (WritePR(RAddress, PRValue))
            {
                bReturn = true;
            }
            else
            {
                if (ConnectToRobot())
                {
                    bReturn = WritePR(RAddress, PRValue);
                }
            }
            if (bReturn == false)
            {
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1634");
            }
            return bReturn;
        }



        private bool WriteR(int RAddress, double RValue)
        {
            try
            {
                return RobotZebra.WriteR(RAddress, RValue); ;
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        public bool WriteToRobotR(int RAddress, double RValue)
        {
            bool bReturn = false;
            if (WriteR(RAddress, RValue))
            {
                bReturn = true;
            }
            else
            {
                if (ConnectToRobot())
                {
                    bReturn = WriteR(RAddress, RValue);
                }
                else
                {
                    bReturn = false;
                }
            }
            if (bReturn == false)
            {
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1634");
            }
            return bReturn;
        }



        private bool ReadPR(int RAddress)
        {
            try
            {
                return RobotZebra.ReadPR(RAddress, ref ReadRobotPR); ;
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        private bool ReadFromRobotPR(int RAddress)
        {
            bool bReturn = false;
            if (ReadPR(RAddress))
            {
                bReturn = true;
            }
            else
            {
                if (ConnectToRobot())
                {
                    bReturn = ReadPR(RAddress);
                }
            }
            if (bReturn == false)
            {
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1634");
            }
            return bReturn;
        }



        public bool ConnectBarcode()
        {

            if (KeyenceBarcode.ConnectStatus())
            {
                return true;
            }
            else
            {
                KeyenceBarcode.Disconnect();
                string IP = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "ScannerIP");//PSet.BardodeIP
                string Port = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "ScannerPort");//PSet.BardodeIP
                int iPort = Convert.ToInt32(Port);//PSet.BarcodePort
                KeyenceBarcode.Connect(IP, iPort);
                Client.BackColor = Color.Green;
                return true;
            }
        }

        public bool ConnectBarcode2()
        {

            if (KeyenceBarcode2.ConnectStatus())
            {
                return true;
            }
            else
            {
                KeyenceBarcode2.Disconnect();
                string IP = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "ScannerIP2");//PSet.BardodeIP
                string Port = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "ScannerPort2");//PSet.BardodeIP
                int iPort = Convert.ToInt32(Port);//PSet.BarcodePort
                KeyenceBarcode2.Connect(IP, iPort);
                Client2.BackColor = Color.Green;
                return true;
            }
        }

        public void Client_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            ConnectBarcode();
            if (KeyenceBarcode.ConnectStatus())
            {
                Client.BackColor = Color.Green;
            }
            else
            {
                Client.BackColor = Color.Red;
            }

        }

        public bool TrigerBarcode()
        {
            RefreshDifferentThreadUI(tbMassage, () =>
            {
                tbMassage.Text = "";
            });

            KeyenceBarcode.Receive();
            if (!KeyenceBarcode.ConnectStatus())
            {
                ConnectBarcode();
            }
            if (KeyenceBarcode.ConnectStatus())
            {
                KeyenceBarcode.Sent("LON" + "\r\n");
                Thread.Sleep(500);
                RefreshDifferentThreadUI(tbMassage, () =>
                 {
                     tbMassage.Text = KeyenceBarcode.Receive();
                 });
            }
            return true;
        }

        private bool TrigerBarcode1InFlow()
        {

            if (KeyenceBarcode.ConnectStatus())
            {
                if (!KeyenceBarcode.isIdle)
                {
                    try
                    {
                        int length = KeyenceBarcode.tcpClient.Client.Available;
                        if (length > 0)
                        {
                            byte[] recived = new byte[length];
                            KeyenceBarcode.tcpClient.Client.Receive(recived);
                        }
                    }
                    catch
                    {

                    }
                    KeyenceBarcode.Sent("LON" + "\r\n");
                    RefreshDifferentThreadUI(MiddleLayer.GantryF.tbMassage, () =>
                    {
                        tbMassage.Text = SysPara.QRCode1;
                        //KeyenceBarcode.isIdle = true;
                        int timer = Convert.ToInt16(GetRecipeValue("RSet", "scantimer"));
                        DelayMs(timer);
                        SysPara.QRCode1 = (KeyenceBarcode.Receive().Replace("\r", "")).Replace("\n", "");
                    });
                    KeyenceBarcode.Sent("LOFF" + "\r\n");
                    //aaron
                    //DelayMs(3500);
                    //KeyenceBarcode.Sent("LOFF" + "\r\n");
                    //DelayMs(100);
                }
                
                RefreshDifferentThreadUI(MiddleLayer.GantryF.tbMassage, () =>
                {
                    tbMassage.Text = SysPara.QRCode1;
                });
                //KeyenceBarcode.Sent("LOFF" + "\r\n");
                processData.fnShowSerial(SysPara.QRCode1);
                KeyenceBarcode.isIdle = false;

                if (SysPara.QRCode1 == "Null")
                    return false;
                return true;
            }
            else
            {
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1300");
                return false;
            }

        }

        public bool TrigerBarcode2()
        {
            RefreshDifferentThreadUI(tbMassage2, () =>
            {
                tbMassage2.Text = "";
            });

            KeyenceBarcode2.Receive();
            if (!KeyenceBarcode2.ConnectStatus())
            {
                ConnectBarcode2();
            }
            if (KeyenceBarcode2.ConnectStatus())
            {
                KeyenceBarcode2.Sent("LON" + "\r\n");
                Thread.Sleep(1000);
                RefreshDifferentThreadUI(tbMassage2, () =>
                {
                    tbMassage2.Text = KeyenceBarcode2.Receive();
                });
            }
            return true;
        }

        private void btServer_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)

        }

        private void button12_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            DialogResult reult = MessageBox.Show("Sure Delete Calib Point?", "", MessageBoxButtons.YesNo, MessageBoxIcon.None, MessageBoxDefaultButton.Button1);
            if (reult == DialogResult.No)
            {
                return;
            }
            if (dgv_H2_VisCalib.CurrentRow != null)
            {
                DataTable dt = SettingData.Tables["VisCalib"];
                dt.Rows.RemoveAt(dgv_H2_VisCalib.CurrentRow.Index);
            }
        }

        public bool Trigger(bool State)
        {
            bool ret = false;
            if (!serialPort2.IsOpen)
            {
                ret = false;
                return ret;
            }
            if (State)
            {
                ret = true;
                serialPort2.Write("READ[OD][OA]" + "\r\n");
                Thread.Sleep(200);
            }
            else
            {

            }
            return ret;
        }

        public bool Trigger2(bool State)
        {
            bool ret = false;
            if (!serialPort3.IsOpen)
            {
                ret = false;
                return ret;
            }
            if (State)
            {
                ret = true;
                serialPort3.Write("READ[OD][OA]" + "\r\n");
                Thread.Sleep(200);
            }
            else
            {

            }
            return ret;
        }

        private void timer1_Tick(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (MiddleLayer.SystemF.IB_FrontAutoOrManualSwich.On())
            {
                if (!bt_ReadIO.Enabled)
                {
                    bt_ReadIO.Enabled = true;
                    bt_WriteIO.Enabled = true;
                }
            }
            else
            {
                if (bt_ReadIO.Enabled)
                {
                    bt_ReadIO.Enabled = false;
                    bt_WriteIO.Enabled = false;
                }
            }

            //SysPara.Frm_TempList.UpdateList(MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MATERIALs);
            if (PFClient.TighteningResultUpdated)
            {
                //FasternDataGetResult = true;
                //textBox2.Text = PFClient.LastTighteningResult.TORQUE.ToString("F3");
                //textBox3.Text = PFClient.LastTighteningResult.ANGLE.ToString("F3");
                //PFClient.TighteningResultUpdated = false;
            }
            else
            {
                if (bConnect && bComStart && bSubscribe)
                {
                    PFKeepAlive();
                }
            }
            if (PFClient2.TighteningResultUpdated2)
            {
                //FasternDataGetResult = true;
                //textBox2.Text = PFClient.LastTighteningResult.TORQUE.ToString("F3");
                //textBox3.Text = PFClient.LastTighteningResult.ANGLE.ToString("F3");
                //PFClient.TighteningResultUpdated = false;
            }
            else
            {
                if (bConnect2 && bComStart2 && bSubscribe2)
                {
                    PFKeepAlive2();
                }
            }

            //bool r1 = IB_Robot_AbnormalAlarm.On();
            //bool r2 = IB_Robot_BatteryAlarm.On();
            //bool r3 = IB_Screw_Alarm.On();
            //bool r4 = IB_Feeder_Alarm.On();
            //bool r5 = !MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_TopCVY_Alarm.On();
            //bool r6 = !MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_BottomCVY_Alarm.On();
            ////bool r7 = MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_Device_Alarm.On();
            ////bool r8 = MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_Device_Lack.On();
            //bool r9 = IB_Screw_Alarm2.On();
            //bool r10 = IB_Feeder_Alarm2.On();

            //if(EnableRobot)
            //{


            //    if (r1)
            //    {
            //            SysPara.NPShowAlarm("5040");
            //    }
            //    if (r2)
            //    {
            //        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("5041");
            //    }
            //    if (r3)
            //    {
            //        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("5042");
            //    }
            //    if (r4)
            //    {
            //        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("5043");
            //    }
            //    if (r5)
            //    {
            //        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("5044");
            //    }
            //    if (r6)
            //    {
            //        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("5045");
            //    }
            //    //if (r7)

        public async void WriteMes(string sSerialNumber, bool bStatusOK)
        {
            SysPara.MESPara = new MESParameter();
            int length = SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count;
            for (int i = 0; i < length; i++)
            {
                Product_Pallet_Code temp = new Product_Pallet_Code();
                temp = SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Dequeue();
                if (!temp.PressureResult)
                {
                    KeyValuePair<string, string> keyTemp = new KeyValuePair<string, string>("  Assemb pressure" + (i + 1).ToString() + "_FAIL", "PalletCode: " + temp.PalletCode + "  ProductCode: " + temp.ProdcutCode + "  product_scan_result: " + temp.productscanresult + "  product_cammer_result: " + temp.productcammerresult);
                    SysPara.MESPara.fails.Add(keyTemp);
                }
                Measurement measureData = new Measurement();
                measureData.measureLabel = "  Pressure" + (i + 1).ToString();
                measureData.measureData = "  PalletCode" + ": " + temp.PalletCode + "  " + "ProductCode" + ": " + temp.ProdcutCode;
                measureData.measureMessage = temp.PressureResult == true ? "Assemb pressure" + (i + 1).ToString() + "_PASS" : "Assemb pressure" + (i + 1).ToString() + "_FAIL";
                SysPara.MESPara.measurements.Add(measureData);
            }
            await MiddleLayer.MesF.WriteUnitMES(sSerialNumber, null, null, true, bStatusOK);
        }


        public async void WriteMesByOne(string sSerialNumber, int index, bool bStatusOK, string result)
        {
            SysPara.MESPara = new MESParameter();

            //Product_Pallet_Code temp = new Product_Pallet_Code();
            //temp = SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Dequeue();
            if (!bStatusOK)
            {
                KeyValuePair<string, string> keyTemp = new KeyValuePair<string, string>("  Assemb pressure" + (index).ToString() + "_FAIL", "PalletCode: " + sSerialNumber + "  product_cammer_result: " + result);// + "  ProductCode: " + temp.ProdcutCode 
                SysPara.MESPara.fails.Add(keyTemp);
            }
            Measurement measureData = new Measurement();
            measureData.measureLabel = "  Pressure" + (index).ToString();
            measureData.measureData = "  PalletCode" + ": " + sSerialNumber;// + "  " + "ProductCode" + ": " + temp.ProdcutCode
            measureData.measureMessage = bStatusOK == true ? "Assemb pressure" + (index).ToString() + "_PASS" : "Assemb pressure" + (index).ToString() + "_FAIL";
            SysPara.MESPara.measurements.Add(measureData);

            await MiddleLayer.MesF.WriteUnitMES(sSerialNumber, null, null, true, bStatusOK);
        }

        private void ResetRobot()
        {
            if (ConnectEpsonRobot())
            {
                OB_RobotPause.Off();
                OB_RobotStart.Off();
                OB_RobotProg1.Off();
                OB_RobotProg2.Off();
                OB_RobotProg3.Off();
                OB_RobotStop.Off();
                OB_RobotContinue.Off();
                OB_RobotReset.Off();

                OB_RobotStop.On();
                DelayMs(500);
                OB_RobotStop.Off();
                OB_RobotReset.On();
                DelayMs(500);
                OB_RobotReset.Off();
            }
        }
        private void startRobot()
        {
            if (IB_RobotReady.On())
            {
                OB_RobotStart.On();
                DelayMs(1000);
                OB_RobotStart.Off();
            }
        }

        public bool SendAutoCalibration(int index)
        {

            RobotPose[0] = Convert.ToDouble(txtPickPosRobotX.Text);
            RobotPose[1] = Convert.ToDouble(txtPickPosRobotY.Text);
            RobotPose[2] = Convert.ToDouble(txtPickPosRobotU.Text);

            switch (index)
            {
                case 1:
                    RobotPose[0] = RobotPose[0] - 5; RobotPose[1] = RobotPose[1] - 5; RobotPose[2] = RobotPose[2] - 0;
                    return Robot.WriteToEpson("014", (100 + index).ToString("FFF"), "FF", 30000, "-05.000", "-05.000", "000.000");//去标定位置1
                case 2:
                    RobotPose[0] = RobotPose[0] - 0; RobotPose[1] = RobotPose[1] - 5; RobotPose[2] = RobotPose[2] - 0;
                    return Robot.WriteToEpson("014", (100 + index).ToString("FFF"), "FF", 30000, "000.000", "-05.000", "000.000");//去标定位置1
                case 3:
                    RobotPose[0] = RobotPose[0] + 5; RobotPose[1] = RobotPose[1] - 5; RobotPose[2] = RobotPose[2] - 0;
                    return Robot.WriteToEpson("014", (100 + index).ToString("FFF"), "FF", 30000, "005.000", "-05.000", "000.000");//去标定位置1
                case 4:
                    RobotPose[0] = RobotPose[0] + 5; RobotPose[1] = RobotPose[1] - 0; RobotPose[2] = RobotPose[2] - 0;
                    return Robot.WriteToEpson("014", (100 + index).ToString("FFF"), "FF", 30000, "005.000", "000.000", "000.000");//去标定位置1
                case 5:
                    RobotPose[0] = RobotPose[0] + 0; RobotPose[1] = RobotPose[1] - 0; RobotPose[2] = RobotPose[2] - 0;
                    return Robot.WriteToEpson("014", (100 + index).ToString("FFF"), "FF", 30000, "000.000", "000.000", "000.000");//去标定位置1
                case 6:
                    RobotPose[0] = RobotPose[0] - 5; RobotPose[1] = RobotPose[1] - 0; RobotPose[2] = RobotPose[2] - 0;
                    return Robot.WriteToEpson("014", (100 + index).ToString("FFF"), "FF", 30000, "-05.000", "000.000", "000.000");//去标定位置1
                case 7:
                    RobotPose[0] = RobotPose[0] - 5; RobotPose[1] = RobotPose[1] + 5; RobotPose[2] = RobotPose[2] - 0;
                    return Robot.WriteToEpson("014", (100 + index).ToString("FFF"), "FF", 30000, "-05.000", "005.000", "000.000");//去标定位置1
                case 8:
                    RobotPose[0] = RobotPose[0] - 0; RobotPose[1] = RobotPose[1] + 5; RobotPose[2] = RobotPose[2] - 0;
                    return Robot.WriteToEpson("014", (100 + index).ToString("FFF"), "FF", 30000, "000.000", "005.000", "000.000");//去标定位置1
                case 9:
                    RobotPose[0] = RobotPose[0] + 5; RobotPose[1] = RobotPose[1] + 5; RobotPose[2] = RobotPose[2] - 0;
                    return Robot.WriteToEpson("014", (100 + index).ToString("FFF"), "FF", 30000, "005.000", "005.000", "000.000");//去标定位置1
                case 10:
                    RobotPose[0] = RobotPose[0] + 0; RobotPose[1] = RobotPose[1] + 0; RobotPose[2] = RobotPose[2] - 6;
                    return Robot.WriteToEpson("014", (100 + index).ToString("FFF"), "FF", 30000, "000.000", "000.000", "-06.000");//去标定位置1
                case 11:
                    RobotPose[0] = RobotPose[0] + 0; RobotPose[1] = RobotPose[1] + 0; RobotPose[2] = RobotPose[2] - 3;
                    return Robot.WriteToEpson("014", (100 + index).ToString("FFF"), "FF", 30000, "000.000", "000.000", "-03.000");//去标定位置1
                case 12:
                    RobotPose[0] = RobotPose[0] + 0; RobotPose[1] = RobotPose[1] + 0; RobotPose[2] = RobotPose[2] + 3;
                    return Robot.WriteToEpson("014", (100 + index).ToString("FFF"), "FF", 30000, "000.000", "000.000", "003.000");//去标定位置1
                case 13:
                    RobotPose[0] = RobotPose[0] + 0; RobotPose[1] = RobotPose[1] + 0; RobotPose[2] = RobotPose[2] + 6;
                    return Robot.WriteToEpson("014", (100 + index).ToString("FFF"), "FF", 30000, "000.000", "000.000", "006.000");//去标定位置1
                default:
                    return true;
            }
        }

        private FCResultType fc_InitialStart_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            initialthrowgripper = 0;
            QRmm = 0;
            QRma = 0;
            throwingjudge = false;
            traycammerposition = 0;
            needNGThrowingMaterial = false;
            NGGripperJudge = 0;
            curR2HaveNum1 = 0;
            //  SysPara.NGpositionCount = 0;
            JudgeGripperuseful = 0;
            Gripperuseful = "ok";
            needThrowing = 0;
            needThrowingcode = "ok";
            bInitialOk = false;
            isServoAlm = false;
            CCDAlogrithmStatus = false;
            isPickProduceOK = true;
            curPalletCode = "";
            curR1HaveNum = 0;
            curR2HaveNum1 = 0;
            SysPara.RIniRet = false;
            SysPara.JAGStarytEvent.Reset();
            SysPara.OverPressure = false;
            SysPara.Product_Result_XYU.Clear();
            CCDReuslt_EachLoop.Clear();//LFF-0209
            SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Clear();
            SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Clear();
            SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Clear();
            SysPara.R1GrabCompeletStatus = false;            //R1 Grab feeder product compelet flag
            SysPara.ScanCount = 0;                            //feeder scan count
            SysPara.R1ScanResult = false;                    //scan state
            MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.NotifyAssembly = false;
            //SysPara.R1AssembStatus = false;                  //pallet call robot can assemb
            SysPara.ProductCount = 12;                        //assemb all count
            SysPara.FeederProductCount = 48;                        //feeder product count
            SysPara.R1AssembResult = false;                  //assemb result
            //SysPara.R1AssembCompeletStatus = false;      //R1 Assemb compelet flag
            MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.AssembCompeletStatus = false;
            SysPara.PressureCompelet = false;
            curProductCount = 0;
            scanCount = 0;
            haveSendCodeToMES = false;
            //SysPara.pallettransferout = true;
            return FCResultType.NEXT;

        }
        //connect scanner
        private FCResultType flowChart1_2_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            bool status1 = ConnectBarcode();
            if (status1)
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "Scanner connect OK", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "Scanner connect NG", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                if (!status1) { SysPara.NPShowAlarm("90010"); }
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }

        }
        //close ccd light
        private FCResultType flowChart1_3_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            OB_RobotLight.Off();
            MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "open Robot Light", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
            if (H1_PickupPos.TakePicture() && H1_DownCCDPickup.TakePicture() && H1_DischargePos.TakePicture())
            {
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1200");
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }

        }

        //Reset Robot
        private FCResultType flowChart1_5_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (ConnectEpsonRobot())
            {
                OB_RobotPause.Off();
                OB_RobotStart.Off();
                OB_RobotProg1.Off();
                OB_RobotProg2.Off();
                OB_RobotProg3.Off();
                OB_RobotStop.Off();
                OB_RobotContinue.Off();
                OB_RobotReset.Off();

                OB_RobotStop.On();
                DelayMs(500);
                OB_RobotStop.Off();
                OB_RobotReset.On();
                DelayMs(500);
                OB_RobotReset.Off();

                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "Reset Robot", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {
                SDKKernal.ShowAlarm("1634");
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
        }
        //Start Robot
        private FCResultType flowChart1_6_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (IB_RobotReady.On())
            {
                OB_RobotStart.On();
                DelayMs(1000);
                OB_RobotStart.Off();
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "start robot", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }
        // wait reset robot compelet
        private FCResultType flowChart1_7_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            DelayMs(1000);
            if (IB_RobotBusy.On()) { MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "Reset Robot ok", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog); return FCResultType.NEXT; }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }
        //goto safety
        private FCResultType flowChart1_8_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            string[] pos = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonPosition, "Goto Safety");
            MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot go safety", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
            if (Robot.WriteToEpson(pos[2], pos[3], pos[4], 30000))
            {
                InitTM.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }

        //wait goto safety
        private FCResultType flowChart6_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            int ret = Robot.ReadFromEpson();
            if (ret == 1)
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot to safety ok", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else if (ret == 0)
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonErrInfo, Robot.ErorrCode)[1] + "," + GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonErrInfo, Robot.ErorrCode)[3], SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                //SysPara.NPShowAlarm("15010");
                return FCResultType.CASE2;
                //SysPara.NPShowAlarm("15009");
                //return FCResultType.CASE1;
            }
            else if (InitTM.On(SysPara.TimeOutSec))
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot to safety timeout", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1635"); return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart1_4_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            OB_FeederStart.Off();
            OB_FeederAlmReset.Off();
            OB_FeederCompleted.Off();
            OB_FeederSafetySignal.On();
            OB_FeederEmgStop.Off();
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }
        //feeder is Alarm
        private FCResultType flowChart7_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (MiddleLayer.SystemF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "DisableFeeder"))
            {
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            if (IB_FeederEmergencyStop.On())
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "fedder emergency stop", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1101");                                                    //fedder emergency stop
                reintento.Dispose();
                reintento = new Reintento();
                reintento.fnSetTextMessageNShow("confrim feeder whether is ready,yes or no", true, false, true);
                if (reintento.dResult == DialogResult.Yes)
                {
                    return FCResultType.IDLE;
                }
                else
                {
                    return FCResultType.IDLE;
                }
            }
            else
            {
                if (IB_FeederAlarm.On())
                {
                    MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "fedder alarm", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                    InitTM.Restart();
                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1100");
                    return FCResultType.CASE1;
                }
                else { MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "fedder not alarm", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog); OB_FeederSafetySignal.On(); return FCResultType.NEXT; }
            }
        }
        //feeder reset
        private FCResultType flowChart9_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            OB_FeederAlmReset.On();
            if (InitTM.On(5000)) { OB_FeederAlmReset.Off(); return FCResultType.NEXT; }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }
        // Initialok
        private FCResultType flowChart8_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            SysPara.EnableMes = MiddleLayer.MesF2.GetSettingValue("PSet", "Enable_Mes");
            MESLib.CommParas.EnableMes = SysPara.EnableMes;
            if (!MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MesInitE)
            {
                MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MesInit();
                MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MesInitE = true;
            }

            //MESLib.CommParas.MesManager._connector.MDLN = "Bottom Cover Loader #01";

            string EQPName = MiddleLayer.MesF2.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EQPName");
            MESLib.CommParas.MesManager._connector.MDLN = EQPName;

            MESLib.CommParas.IP = MiddleLayer.MesF2.GetSettingValue("PSet", "Mes_IP");
            MESLib.CommParas.Port = MiddleLayer.MesF2.GetSettingValue("PSet", "Mes_Port");

            if (SysPara.EnableMes)
            {
                // secgem
                MESLib.CommFuns.StartSecsGem(out string error);
                if (error != string.Empty)
                {
                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70105"); // secgem open fail
                    MESLib.CommParas.MesCenter.PauseRunning();
                    return FCResultType.IDLE;
                }
                System.Threading.Thread.Sleep(1500);
                if (!MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MesConnect(MESLib.CommParas.IP, MESLib.CommParas.Port))
                {
                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70109"); // secgem open fail
                    //System.Threading.Thread.Sleep(1000);
                    //MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MesClose();
                    return FCResultType.IDLE;
                }

            }

            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }
        #endregion
        #region AutoRun
        private FCResultType flowChart10_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            FeederT.Restart();
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }
        //Waiting for Feeder incoming
        private FCResultType flowChart11_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "IsDryCycleMode"))
            { return FCResultType.NEXT; }
            DelayMs(300);
            FeederImages.Clear();
            // check the 1st product in the pallet
            if (SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count == 0 && SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count == 0)
            {
                SysPara.PalletProductCount = 0;
                MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.InitMesParas();

                for (int i = 0; i <
                MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PRODUCEDs.Length; i++)
                {
                    MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PRODUCEDs[i] = true; // default set all position ok
                }
            }
            // every new picking
            MaterialID_Array = new string[3];

            if (SysPara.ScanCount >= (Int32)GetRecipeValue("RSet", "CoverAssembCount"))
            {
                SysPara.JAGStarytEvent.Set();
                FeederT.Restart();
                SysPara.ScanCount = 0;
            }
            if (SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count == 0)
            {
                SysPara.ScanCount = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "FeederPickIndex");//LFF-0209   SysPara.FeederProductCount
                if (SysPara.ScanCount % 3 != 0)
                {
                    SysPara.ScanCount = SysPara.ScanCount + 3 - SysPara.ScanCount % 3;



                    //SysPara.ScanCount++;
                    DataTable dt = RecipeData.Tables["RSet"];
                    dt.Rows[0]["FeederPickIndex"] = SysPara.ScanCount;
                    dt.AcceptChanges();
                    this.WriteRecipeData(string.Format("{0}\\{1}.xml", SysPara.RecipeDataDirectory, SysPara.RecipeName));

                }


            }
            if (GetSettingValue("MSet", "EPlaningCounts"))
            {
                int Planing = GetSettingValue("MSet", "ProQuantity");
                int finished = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "finishedCounts");
                if (Planing == finished)
                {

                    return FCResultType.IDLE;
                }
                if (Planing < finished)
                {
                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("2029");
                    return FCResultType.IDLE;
                }

            }
            if ((!FeederT.On(5000)) && SysPara.ScanCount == 0)
            {
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }



            if (IB_FeederInPlace.On() || MiddleLayer.SystemF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "DisableFeeder"))
            {
                lock (SysPara.StatusLock)
                {
                    SysPara.R1GrabCompeletStatus = false;
                }
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart12_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            SysPara.ScanCount = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "FeederPickIndex");//LFF-0209   SysPara.FeederProductCount
            if (SysPara.ScanCount >= (Int32)GetRecipeValue("RSet", "CoverAssembCount")) { SysPara.ScanCount = 0; }
            SysPara.ScanCount++;
            DataTable dt = RecipeData.Tables["RSet"];
            dt.Rows[0]["FeederPickIndex"] = SysPara.ScanCount;
            dt.AcceptChanges();
            this.WriteRecipeData(string.Format("{0}\\{1}.xml", SysPara.RecipeDataDirectory, SysPara.RecipeName));
            MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "feeder scan index: " + SysPara.ScanCount, SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }
        //R1 Goto scan feeder 
        private FCResultType flowChart13_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            string[] pos = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonPosition, "Feeder product ScanPosition");
            MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "Command：" + pos[2] + "Pos：" + string.Format("{0:d3}", SysPara.ScanCount) + "AppIndex：" + pos[4], SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);

            if (Robot.WriteToEpson(pos[2], string.Format("{0:d3}", SysPara.ScanCount), pos[4], 30000)) { RunTM.Restart(); return FCResultType.NEXT; }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart14_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            int ret = Robot.ReadFromEpson();
            if (ret == 1)
            {
                RunTM.Restart();
                ScanExposure = 0.5;
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot to scan product position ok", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog); return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else if (ret == 0)
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonErrInfo, Robot.ErorrCode)[1], SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            else if (RunTM.On(SysPara.TimeOutSec))
            { MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot to scan product position timeout", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog); SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1635"); return FCResultType.IDLE; }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }

        public string[] MaterialID_Array = new string[3];


        //TriggerScan
        private FCResultType flowChart15_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            PalletImg.Clear();
            if (MiddleLayer.SystemF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "DisableProductCodeScan"))
            {
                SysPara.R1ScanResult = true;
                SysPara.QRCode1 = "null";
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "product scan :" + SysPara.R1ScanResult + ":" + SysPara.QRCode1, SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            if (MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "IsDryCycleMode"))
            {
                SysPara.R1ScanResult = true;
                SysPara.QRCode1 = "null";
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "product scan :" + SysPara.R1ScanResult + ":" + SysPara.QRCode1, SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            if (TrigerBarcode1InFlow())
            {
                string[] ProductCode = SysPara.QRCode1.Split(',');
                if (ProductCode.Length == 4)
                {
                    SysPara.R1ScanResult = true;
                    SysPara.QRCode1 = ProductCode[3];
                    if (SysPara.QRCode1 == "ERROR")
                    {
                        SysPara.R1ScanResult = false;
                        SysPara.QRCode1 = "ERROR";
                    }
                    MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "product scan :" + SysPara.R1ScanResult + SysPara.QRCode1, SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
                else
                {
                    if (!RunTM.On(5000))
                    {
                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }
                    SysPara.R1ScanResult = false;
                    SysPara.QRCode1 = "ERROR";
                    MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "product scan :" + SysPara.R1ScanResult + SysPara.QRCode1, SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
            }
            else
            {
                if (!RunTM.On(5000))
                {
                    return FCResultType.IDLE;
                }
                SysPara.R1ScanResult = false;
                SysPara.QRCode1 = "ERROR";
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("90022");
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "product scan :" + SysPara.R1ScanResult + SysPara.QRCode1, SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }

        }
        private FCResultType flowChart16_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (SysPara.QRCode1 == "ERROR")
            {
                SysPara.R1ScanResult = false;
            }
            if (SysPara.R1ScanResult)
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "scan ok", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                ProductCode_PickPos code_PickPos = new ProductCode_PickPos();                    //scan success，Record productCode and Index
                code_PickPos.Code = SysPara.QRCode1;
                code_PickPos.PosIndex = SysPara.ScanCount;
                SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Enqueue(code_PickPos);
                MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MATERIALs[SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count + SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count - 1] = SysPara.QRCode1;
                MaterialID_Array[SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count - 1] = SysPara.QRCode1;
            }
            else if (!SysPara.R1ScanResult)                                                                                //if pick fail,replace the first
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "scan ng continue", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                return FCResultType.CASE2;
            }
            //int productCount = 0;
            //if (((Int16)GetRecipeValue("RSet", "PalletAssembCount") - SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count) / 3 != 0)
            //{
            //    productCount = 3;
            //}
            //else
            //{
            //    productCount = ((Int16)GetRecipeValue("RSet", "PalletAssembCount") - SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count);
            //}
            if (GetSettingValue("MSet", "EPlaningCounts"))
            {
                int Planing = GetSettingValue("MSet", "ProQuantity");
                int finished = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "finishedCounts");
                if ((Planing - finished) < 3)
                {
                    if (SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count == (Planing - finished))
                    {
                        for (int i = SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count; i < 3; i++)
                        {
                            ProductCode_PickPos code_PickPos = new ProductCode_PickPos();
                            code_PickPos.Code = "ScanerNG";
                            code_PickPos.PosIndex = SysPara.ScanCount;
                            SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Enqueue(code_PickPos);
                            SysPara.ScanCount++;
                        }
                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    }
                }
            }
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        //R1 Goto Product CCD
        private FCResultType flowChart17_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            OB_RobotLight.On();

            string[] pos = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonPosition, "Feeder product CamPosition");
            //ProductCode_PickPos code_PickPos = new ProductCode_PickPos();

            MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "Command：" + pos[2] + "Pos：" + string.Format("{0:d3}", SysPara.ScanCount) + "AppIndex：" + pos[4], SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);

        }
        //Wait R1 on CCDPos
        private FCResultType flowChart18_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            int ret = Robot.ReadFromEpson();
            if (ret == 1) { MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot to cammer position ok", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog); return FCResultType.NEXT; }
            else if (ret == 0)
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonErrInfo, Robot.ErorrCode)[1], SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            else if (RunTM.On(SysPara.TimeOutSec))
            { MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot to cammer position timeout", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog); SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1635"); return FCResultType.IDLE; }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }
        //CCD take picture
        int scanAttempt = 0;
        private FCResultType flowChart19_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            SysPara.visionPos = new VisionPos();
            SysPara.visionposition = new VisionPosition();

            ProductCode_PickPos code_PickPos = new ProductCode_PickPos();
            //if (SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count > 0) { code_PickPos = SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.ToList()[curR2HaveNum1]; }

            //nowpickX = Convert.ToDouble(GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_RobotPosition, "FeederPickCamPos" + code_PickPos.PosIndex.ToString())[2]);
            //nowpickY = Convert.ToDouble(GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_RobotPosition, "FeederPickCamPos" + code_PickPos.PosIndex.ToString())[3]);
            pickcamindex = SysPara.ScanCount % 3;
            if (pickcamindex==0)
            {
                pickcamindex = 3;
            }

            H1_PickupPos.TB.Inputs["AxisXPos"].Value = robotPos[0];
            H1_PickupPos.TB.Inputs["AxisYPos"].Value = robotPos[1];
            H1_PickupPos.TB.Inputs["ClawIndex"].Value = Convert.ToInt32(pickcamindex);

            try
            {
                double exposure = GetSettingValue("MSet", "PickExpTime");

                if (H1_PickupPos.TakePicture(exposure))                              //CCD take picture ok
                {
                    H1_PickupPos.TB.Inputs["AngleLimit"].Value = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "AngleLimit");
                    H1_PickupPos.RunTB();
                    RefreshDifferentThreadUI(cogRecDisp_H5_Recipe, () =>
                    {
                        cogRecDisp_H5_Recipe.StaticGraphics.Clear();
                        cogRecDisp_H5_Recipe.Image = H1_PickupPos.TB.Outputs["OutputImage"].Value as CogImage8Grey;
                        cogRecDisp_H5_Recipe.Record = H1_PickupPos.TB.CreateLastRunRecord().SubRecords[0];
                        cogRecDisp_H5_Recipe.Fit();
                    });
                    #region save img
                    try
                    {
                        string isAccept = H1_PickupPos.IsAccept ? "OK\\" : "NG\\";

                        if (MiddleLayer.GantryF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EnableSaveOriginalImg"))
                        {
                            string path1 = @"D:\ImgSave\ProductImg\Original\" + DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMdd") + "\\" + isAccept;
                            string imgname = DateTime.Now.ToString("HH_mm_ss_fff") + "_" + SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.ToList()[curR2HaveNum1].Code + ".bmp";
                            using (CogImageFileBMP img = new CogImageFileBMP())
                            {
                                if (!Directory.Exists(path1)) { Directory.CreateDirectory(path1); }
                                img.Open(path1 + imgname, CogImageFileModeConstants.Write);
                                img.Append(H1_PickupPos.InputImage);
                                img.Close();
                            }
                        }
                        SaveImage image = new SaveImage();
                        image.FeederImage = cogRecDisp_H5_Recipe.CreateContentBitmap(CogDisplayContentBitmapConstants.Display);
                        image.FeederTimer = DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMddHHmmss");
                        FeederImages.Add(image);

                    }
                    catch (Exception)
                    {

                    }
                    #endregion
                    if (!MiddleLayer.SystemF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "DisableVision"))              //normal 
                    {
                        if (H1_PickupPos.IsAccept)                                              //visionpro no error
                        {
                            string CoverBarcode = (string)H1_PickupPos.TB.Outputs["DecodedString"].Value;

                            if (CoverBarcode == "" || CoverBarcode == "ERROR")
                            {
                                scanAttempt++;
                                if(scanAttempt == 3)
                                {
                                    scanAttempt = 0;
                                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1301", "Failed To Scan with CCD");
                                    CCDAlogrithmStatus = false;
                                    CCDReuslt_EachLoop.Add(CCDAlogrithmStatus);
                                    return FCResultType.CASE2;
                                }
                                return FCResultType.IDLE;
                            }
                            else
                            {
                                foreach(string st in MaterialID_Array)
                                {
                                    if (st == CoverBarcode)
                                    {
                                        scanAttempt = 0;
                                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1301", "COVER ID Duplication");
                                        CCDAlogrithmStatus = false;
                                        CCDReuslt_EachLoop.Add(CCDAlogrithmStatus);
                                        return FCResultType.CASE2;
                                    }
                                    
                                }
                                
                            }
                           

                            double a = Convert.ToDouble(H1_PickupPos.TB.Outputs["X"].Value);
                            double b = Convert.ToDouble(H1_PickupPos.TB.Outputs["Y"].Value);
                            double c = Convert.ToDouble(H1_PickupPos.TB.Outputs["Angle"].Value);
                            double d = Convert.ToDouble(H1_PickupPos.TB.Outputs["AngleDiff"].Value);
                            

                            
                       

                            SysPara.QRCode1 = CoverBarcode;
                            SysPara.visionposition.X = Robot.FormatOutput(a);
                            SysPara.visionposition.Y = Robot.FormatOutput(b);
                            SysPara.visionposition.U = Robot.FormatOutput(c);

                            try
                            {
                                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "PosX：" + SysPara.visionposition.X.ToString() + "PosY：" + SysPara.visionposition.Y.ToString(), SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);

                                double max_X = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "FeederMaxValueX");
                                double max_Y = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "FeederMaxValueY");
                                double min_X = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "FeederMinValueX");
                                double min_Y = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "FeederMinValueY");
                                bool bValue = max_X > Convert.ToDouble(SysPara.visionposition.X) && min_X < Convert.ToDouble(SysPara.visionposition.X) &&
                                    max_Y > Convert.ToDouble(SysPara.visionposition.Y) && min_Y < Convert.ToDouble(SysPara.visionposition.Y) ? true : false;
                                if (!bValue)
                                {
                                    SDKKernal.ShowAlarm("1206");
                                    return FCResultType.IDLE;
                                }
                            }
                            catch (Exception)
                            {
                                SDKKernal.ShowAlarm("1206");
                                return FCResultType.IDLE;
                            }
                            CCDAlogrithmStatus = true;
                            CCDReuslt_EachLoop.Add(CCDAlogrithmStatus);
                            MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "cammer result ok", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                            if (SysPara.QRCode1 == "ERROR")
                            {
                                return FCResultType.CASE1;
                            }
                            else
                            {
                                if (GetSettingValue("MSet", "EPlaningCounts"))
                                {
                                    int Planing = GetSettingValue("MSet", "ProQuantity");
                                    int finished = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "finishedCounts");
                                    if ((Planing - finished) < 3)
                                    {
                                        if (SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count == (Planing - finished))
                                        {
                                            for (int i = SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count; i < 3; i++)
                                            {
                                                code_PickPos.Code = "ScanerNG";
                                                code_PickPos.PosIndex = SysPara.ScanCount;
                                                SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Enqueue(code_PickPos);
                                            }
                                            return FCResultType.NEXT;
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "Scan Index:"+ SysPara.ScanCount + ",Scan ok,Barcode:"+ SysPara.QRCode1, SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                                                   //scan success，Record productCode and Index
                                code_PickPos.Code = SysPara.QRCode1;
                                code_PickPos.PosIndex = SysPara.ScanCount;
                                SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Enqueue(code_PickPos);
                                MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MATERIALs[SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count + SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count - 1] = SysPara.QRCode1;
                                MaterialID_Array[SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count - 1] = SysPara.QRCode1;
                            }

                            return FCResultType.NEXT;
                        }
                        else                                                                   //visionpro  has  error
                        {
                            SysPara.visionposition.X = "000.000";
                            SysPara.visionposition.Y = "000.000";
                            SysPara.visionposition.U = "000.000";
                            SysPara.QRCode1 = "ERROR";
                            CCDAlogrithmStatus = false;
                            CCDReuslt_EachLoop.Add(CCDAlogrithmStatus);
                            if (H1_PickupPos.TB.RunStatus.Result == CogToolResultConstants.Reject)
                            {
                                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1211");
                            }
                            MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "cammer result ng", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                            return FCResultType.CASE2;
                        }
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        SysPara.visionposition.X = "000.000";
                        SysPara.visionposition.Y = "000.000";
                        SysPara.visionposition.U = "000.000";
                        SysPara.QRCode1 = "Null123";
                        CCDAlogrithmStatus = true;
                        CCDReuslt_EachLoop.Add(CCDAlogrithmStatus);

                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    CCDAlogrithmStatus = false;
                    CCDReuslt_EachLoop.Add(CCDAlogrithmStatus);
                    SysPara.QRCode1 = "ERROR";
                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1205");        //CCD fail
                    return FCResultType.IDLE;
                }
            }
            catch
            {
                CCDAlogrithmStatus = false;
                CCDReuslt_EachLoop.Add(CCDAlogrithmStatus);
                SysPara.QRCode1 = "ERROR";
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1205");        //CCD fail
                return FCResultType.CASE2;
            }
        }
        //CCD position
        private FCResultType flowChart83_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            ProductCode_PickPos code_PickPos = new ProductCode_PickPos();
            if (SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count > 0) { code_PickPos = SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.ToList()[SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count-1]; }

            nowpickX = Convert.ToDouble(GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_RobotPosition, "FeederPickCamPos" + code_PickPos.PosIndex.ToString())[2]);
            nowpickY = Convert.ToDouble(GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_RobotPosition, "FeederPickCamPos" + code_PickPos.PosIndex.ToString())[3]);


            Product_Result_XYU product_result_xyu_add = new Product_Result_XYU();

            if (code_PickPos.Code != "ScanerNG")
            {
                product_result_xyu_add.PositionIndex = Convert.ToString(code_PickPos.PosIndex);
                product_result_xyu_add.Result = CCDAlogrithmStatus;
                product_result_xyu_add.X = SysPara.visionposition.X;
                product_result_xyu_add.Y = SysPara.visionposition.Y;
                product_result_xyu_add.U = SysPara.visionposition.U;

                SysPara.Product_Result_XYU.Enqueue(product_result_xyu_add);
            }
            else if (code_PickPos.Code == "ScanerNG")
            {
                product_result_xyu_add.PositionIndex = Convert.ToString(code_PickPos.PosIndex);
                product_result_xyu_add.Result = false;
                product_result_xyu_add.X = "000.000";
                product_result_xyu_add.Y = "000.000";
                product_result_xyu_add.U = "000.000";

                SysPara.Product_Result_XYU.Enqueue(product_result_xyu_add);
            }

            //curR2HaveNum1++;
            int productCount = 0;
            if (((Int16)GetRecipeValue("RSet", "PalletAssembCount") - SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count) / 3 != 0)
            {
                productCount = 3;
            }
            else
            {
                productCount = ((Int16)GetRecipeValue("RSet", "PalletAssembCount") - SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count);
            }
            if (SysPara.Product_Result_XYU.Count >= productCount)
            {
                OB_RobotLight.Off();
                curR2HaveNum1 = 0;
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "cammer product had full 3", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            return FCResultType.CASE1;
        }

        //Judge Vision success
        private FCResultType flowChart20_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            Product_Result_XYU product_result_xyu_add = new Product_Result_XYU();
            if (SysPara.Product_Result_XYU.Count > 0)
            {
                product_result_xyu_add = SysPara.Product_Result_XYU.ToList()[curR1HaveNum];
            }

            ProductCode_PickPos[] code_PickPoslist = new ProductCode_PickPos[3];
            if (SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count > 0)
            {
                code_PickPoslist = SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.ToArray();
            }

            if (MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "IsDryCycleMode"))
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "Dry run finish  Check", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                SysPara.SnapPCBCount = 0;
                CCDReuslt_EachLoop.Clear();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {
                if (product_result_xyu_add.Result == true)
                {
                    if (GetSettingValue("MSet", "EPlaningCounts"))
                    {
                        int finished = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "finishedCounts");
                        DataTable dt = RecipeData.Tables["RSet"];
                        dt.Rows[0]["finishedCounts"] = finished + 1;
                        dt.AcceptChanges();
                        this.WriteRecipeData(string.Format("{0}\\{1}.xml", SysPara.RecipeDataDirectory, SysPara.RecipeName));
                    }
                    MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "product ok can take out", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                    OB_FeederSafetySignal.Off();
                    SysPara.SnapPCBCount = 0;
                    CCDReuslt_EachLoop.Clear();
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
                else
                {
                    if (code_PickPoslist[curR1HaveNum].Code == "ScanerNG")
                    {
                        MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "product ScanNG not takeout", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                        return FCResultType.CASE1;
                    }
                    code_PickPoslist[curR1HaveNum].Code = "CammerNG";
                    SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos = new Queue<ProductCode_PickPos>(code_PickPoslist);
                    MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "product CammerNG not takeout", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                    return FCResultType.CASE1;
                }
            }

        }
        //R1 Goto Grab Product
        Stopwatch stopwatch = new Stopwatch();
        private FCResultType flowChart21_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            Product_Result_XYU product_result_xyu_add = new Product_Result_XYU();
            if (SysPara.Product_Result_XYU.Count > 0)
            {
                product_result_xyu_add = SysPara.Product_Result_XYU.ToList()[curR1HaveNum];
            }
            string[] pos = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonPosition, "Feeder Product PickPosition");
            MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "Command：" + pos[2] + "Pos：" + string.Format("{0:d3}", (int)SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.ToList()[curR1HaveNum].PosIndex) + "AppIndex：" + string.Format("{0:d3}",
               curR1HaveNum + 1), SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
            if (Robot.WriteToEpson(pos[2], string.Format("{0:d3}", (int)SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.ToList()[curR1HaveNum].PosIndex), string.Format("{0:d2}",
                curR1HaveNum + 1), 30000, product_result_xyu_add.X, product_result_xyu_add.Y, product_result_xyu_add.U))
            {
                RunTM.Restart();
                if (string.Format("{0:d2}", curR1HaveNum + 1) == "01")
                {
                    MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.processData.textBox1.Text = "0";
                }
                else if (string.Format("{0:d2}", curR1HaveNum + 1) == "02")
                {
                    MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.processData.textBox2.Text = "0";
                }
                else if (string.Format("{0:d2}", curR1HaveNum + 1) == "03")
                {
                    MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.processData.textBox3.Text = "0";
                }
                stopwatch.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }

        //Wait R1 Grab success
        private FCResultType flowChart22_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            int ret = Robot.ReadFromEpson();
            double pickTime = 0;
            if (ret == 1)
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot to takeout product position ok", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                OB_FeederSafetySignal.On();
                stopwatch.Stop();
                pickTime = (double)stopwatch.ElapsedMilliseconds / 1000.00;
                if (string.Format("{0:d2}", curR1HaveNum + 1) == "01")
                {
                    MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.processData.textBox1.Text = pickTime.ToString("f3");
                }
                else if (string.Format("{0:d2}", curR1HaveNum + 1) == "02")
                {
                    MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.processData.textBox2.Text = pickTime.ToString("f3");
                }
                else if (string.Format("{0:d2}", curR1HaveNum + 1) == "03")
                {
                    MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.processData.textBox3.Text = pickTime.ToString("f3");
                }
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else if (ret == 0)
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonErrInfo, Robot.ErorrCode)[1], SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            else if (RunTM.On(SysPara.TimeOutSec))
            { MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot to takeout product position timeout", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog); SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1635"); return FCResultType.IDLE; }        //R1 timeout
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }

        //Judge Grab count=3
        private FCResultType flowChart23_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            if (SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count >= 12)
                bProductAllNG = true;
            curR1HaveNum++;
            int productCount = 0;
            if (((Int16)GetRecipeValue("RSet", "PalletAssembCount") - SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count) / 3 != 0)
            {
                productCount = 3;
            }
            else
            {
                productCount = ((Int16)GetRecipeValue("RSet", "PalletAssembCount") - SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count);
            }//Success，Count+1
            if (curR1HaveNum >= productCount)
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot had takeout product 3", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                SysPara.Product_Result_XYU.Clear();

                if (SysPara.ScanCount >= (Int32)GetRecipeValue("RSet", "CoverAssembCount"))//LFF-0209    SysPara.FeederProductCount)
                {
                    MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot had takeout product 12", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                    SysPara.JAGStarytEvent.Set();                                          //feeder Grab product compelet
                    SysPara.ScanCount = 0;
                    FeederT.Restart();
                }
                curR1HaveNum = 0;
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else { return FCResultType.CASE1; }
        }
