        //TriggerScan
        private FCResultType flowChart31_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            //if (MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "IsDryCycleMode") || !MiddleLayer.ScannerF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "HabilitarModuloScanner"))
            if (MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "IsDryCycleMode"))
            {
                SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Enqueue("LGIT-0N-0A");
                SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Enqueue("LGIT-0N-0B");
                SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Enqueue("LGIT-0N-0C");
                SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Enqueue("LGIT-0N-0D");
                SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Enqueue("LGIT-0N-0E");
                SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Enqueue("LGIT-0N-0F");
                SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Enqueue("LGIT-0N-0G");
                SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Enqueue("LGIT-0N-0H");
                SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Enqueue("LGIT-0N-0I");
                SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Enqueue("LGIT-0N-0J");
                SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Enqueue("LGIT-0N-0K");
                SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Enqueue("LGIT-0N-0L");
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production,
                    "pallet scan :" + SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.ToArray()[0] + "," + SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.ToArray()[1] + "," + SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.ToArray()[2],
                    SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }

            if (TrigerBarcode1InFlow())
            {
                string[] QRcode = SysPara.QRCode1.Split(',');
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production,
                    "pallet scan :" + SysPara.QRCode1, SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                string tempCodeStr = "ERROR";
                // 04/01/2023 
                if (QRcode.Length >= 3)
                {
                    for (int i = 0; i < QRcode.Length; i++)
                    {
                        if (QRcode[i].Contains("LG"))
                        {
                            tempCodeStr = QRcode[i];
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1301");
                    return FCResultType.IDLE;
                }
                if (tempCodeStr == "ERROR")
                {
                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1301");
                    return FCResultType.IDLE;
                }

                // MES TrayQRCode
                string codehead = tempCodeStr.Substring(0, 8);
                for (int i = 0; i < MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.TRAY_POSITION_QRCODEs.Length; i++)
                {
                    MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.TRAY_POSITION_QRCODEs[i] =
                        codehead + (i + 1).ToString("0#");
                    SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Enqueue(MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.TRAY_POSITION_QRCODEs[i]);
                    scanPellatCode.Add(MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.TRAY_POSITION_QRCODEs[i]);
                }
            }
            else
            {
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1301");
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }
        ////Judge Tray code is valuable
        private FCResultType flowChart32_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "IsDryCycleMode"))
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "Judge tray code", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {
                bool EachGroupHasPCBFlag = false;
                if (SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Count >= 3)  // 【修改】至少需要3个料盘码
                {
                    // 【修改】一次取3个料盘码（一组）
                    SysPara.PalletCode1 = SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Dequeue();
                    SysPara.PalletCode2 = SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Dequeue();
                    SysPara.PalletCode3 = SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Dequeue();
                    curPalletCode = SysPara.PalletCode1;  // 保留第一个作为代表

                    int posCount = 12 - SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Count;

                    // 【修改】检查组内3个位置是否有PCB
                    if ((posCount % 3 == 0) && (SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count >= 3))
                    {
                        for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
                        {

                            if (bCheckProduct[posCount + i - 1])
                            {
                                EachGroupHasPCBFlag = true;
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    if (SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count < 3)
                    {
                        EachGroupHasPCBFlag = true;
                    }

                    if (EachGroupHasPCBFlag)
                    {
                        // 【修改】检查组内第一个位置
                        if (SysPara.PalletCode1 != "ERROR" && bCheckProduct[posCount - 1])
                        {
                            MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "this tray code can put product", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                            return FCResultType.NEXT;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "this tray code donot put product", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                            return FCResultType.CASE1;
                        }
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        // 【修改】整组无PCB，创建3个空产品记录（使用3个料盘码）
                        SysPara.Product_Result_XYU.Clear();
                        // 注意：不需要额外的Dequeue，因为已经取了3个码

                        for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
                        {
                            SysPara.ProductPalletCode = new Product_Pallet_Code();
                            SysPara.ProductPalletCode.ProdcutCode = "";
                            // 【修改】每个产品使用对应的料盘码
                            if (i == 0) SysPara.ProductPalletCode.PalletCode = SysPara.PalletCode1;
                            else if (i == 1) SysPara.ProductPalletCode.PalletCode = SysPara.PalletCode2;
                            else SysPara.ProductPalletCode.PalletCode = SysPara.PalletCode3;
                            SysPara.ProductPalletCode.PressureResult = false;
                            SysPara.ProductPalletCode.productscanresult = SysPara.ProductPalletCode.ProdcutCode == "ScanNG" ? "NG" : "OK";
                            SysPara.ProductPalletCode.productcammerresult = SysPara.ProductPalletCode.ProdcutCode == "CammerNG" ? "NG" : "OK";
                            SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Enqueue(SysPara.ProductPalletCode);
                        }
                        MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "not pcb1", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                        return FCResultType.CASE2;
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "not pcb2", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                    return FCResultType.CASE2;
                }
            }
        }
        private FCResultType flowChart71_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            ProductCode_PickPos code_PickPos = new ProductCode_PickPos();

            JudgeGripperuseful = QRma;
            if (SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count > 0)
            {

                code_PickPos = SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.ToList()[JudgeGripperuseful];
            }
            Gripperuseful = code_PickPos.Code;
            int posCount = SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count;    //% 3;


            if (posCount < 12)
            {
                if (Gripperuseful != "ScanerNG" && Gripperuseful != "CammerNG" && bCheckProduct[posCount])
                {
                    MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "this robot gripper" + JudgeGripperuseful + "has product can put product", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
                else
                {
                    MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "this robot gripper" + JudgeGripperuseful + " donot has product donot need put product", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                    return FCResultType.CASE1;
                }
            }
            else
            {
                bProductAllNG = true;
                return FCResultType.CASE2;

            }
        }

        //CCD take picture
        private FCResultType flowChart35_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            SysPara.visionPos = new VisionPos();
            SysPara.visionposition = new VisionPosition();


            try
            {
                double exposure = GetSettingValue("MSet", "DisChargeExpTime");
                if (H1_DischargePos.TakePicture(exposure))    //CCD take picture
                {
                    H1_DischargePos.RunTB();
                    RefreshDifferentThreadUI(cogRecDisp_H5_Recipe, () =>
                    {
                        cogRecDisp_H5_Recipe.StaticGraphics.Clear();
                        cogRecDisp_H5_Recipe.Image = H1_DischargePos.InputImage;
                        cogRecDisp_H5_Recipe.Record = H1_DischargePos.TB.CreateLastRunRecord().SubRecords[0];
                        cogRecDisp_H5_Recipe.Fit();
                    });
                    #region save img
                    try
                    {
                        string isAccept = H1_DischargePos.IsAccept ? "OK\\" : "NG\\";

                        if (MiddleLayer.GantryF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EnableSaveOriginalImg"))
                        {
                            string path1 = @"D:\ImgSave\PalletImg\Original\" + DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMdd") + "\\" + isAccept;
                            string imgname = DateTime.Now.ToString("HH_mm_ss_fff") + "_" + SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.ToList()[curR2HaveNum1].Code + ".bmp";
                            using (CogImageFileBMP img = new CogImageFileBMP())
                            {
                                if (!Directory.Exists(path1)) { Directory.CreateDirectory(path1); }
                                img.Open(path1 + imgname, CogImageFileModeConstants.Write);
                                img.Append(H1_DischargePos.InputImage);
                                img.Close();
                            }
                        }

                        //if (MiddleLayer.GantryF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EnableSaveJPEGImg"))
                        //{
                        //    string path2 = @"D:\ImgSave\PalletImg\Conversion\" + DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMdd") + "\\" + isAccept;
                        //    string imgname1 = DateTime.Now.ToString("HH_mm_ss_fff") + "_" + SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.ToList()[curR2HaveNum1].Code + ".bmp";
                        //    using (CogImageFileBMP img1 = new CogImageFileBMP())
                        //    {
                        //        if (!Directory.Exists(path2)) { Directory.CreateDirectory(path2); }
                        //        Image newimg = cogRecDisp_H5_Recipe.CreateContentBitmap(CogDisplayContentBitmapConstants.Display);
                        //        newimg.Save(path2 + imgname1, System.Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat.Jpeg);
                        //        newimg.Dispose();
                        //    }
                        //}
                        SaveImage image = new SaveImage();
                        image.FeederImage = cogRecDisp_H5_Recipe.CreateContentBitmap(CogDisplayContentBitmapConstants.Display);
                        image.FeederTimer = DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMddHHss");
                        PalletImg.Add(image);



                    }
                    catch (Exception)
                    {

                    }
                    #endregion
                    if (!MiddleLayer.SystemF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "DisableVision"))
                    {

                        if (H1_DischargePos.IsAccept)
                        {
                            double A = Convert.ToDouble(H1_DischargePos.TB.Outputs["X"].Value);
                            double B = Convert.ToDouble(H1_DischargePos.TB.Outputs["Y"].Value);
                            double C = Convert.ToDouble(H1_DischargePos.TB.Outputs["Angle"].Value);

                            SysPara.visionposition.X = Robot.FormatOutput(A);
                            SysPara.visionposition.Y = Robot.FormatOutput(B);
                            SysPara.visionposition.U = Robot.FormatOutput(C);
                            CCDAlogrithmStatus = true;
                            MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot in pallet: " + traycammerposition + 1 + " cammer ok", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                            return FCResultType.NEXT;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            SysPara.visionposition.X = "000.000";
                            SysPara.visionposition.Y = "000.000";
                            SysPara.visionposition.U = "000.000";
                            CCDAlogrithmStatus = false;
                            //SysPara.NPShowAlarm("90021");                                 //CCD Alogrithm fail
                            MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot in pallet: " + traycammerposition + 1 + " cammer ng", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                            return FCResultType.NEXT;
                        }
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        SysPara.visionposition.X = "000.000";
                        SysPara.visionposition.Y = "000.000";
                        SysPara.visionposition.U = "000.000";
                        CCDAlogrithmStatus = true;
                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    }

                }
                else
                {
                    CCDAlogrithmStatus = false;
                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1205");        //CCD fail
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
            }
            catch
            {
                CCDAlogrithmStatus = false;
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1205");        //CCD fail
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
        }
        private FCResultType flowChart84_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            Product_Result_XYU product_result_xyu_add = new Product_Result_XYU();


            ProductCode_PickPos code_PickPos = new ProductCode_PickPos();

            JudgeGripperuseful = QRma;


            if (SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count > 0)
            {

                code_PickPos = SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.ToList()[JudgeGripperuseful];
            }
            Gripperuseful = code_PickPos.Code;


            //nowputX = Convert.ToDouble(GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_RobotPosition, "ConveryAssembPos" + (traycammerposition + 1).ToString())[2]);
            //nowputY = Convert.ToDouble(GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_RobotPosition, "ConveryAssembPos" + (traycammerposition + 1).ToString())[3]);
            //nowputU = Convert.ToDouble(GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_RobotPosition, "ConveryAssembPos" + (traycammerposition + 1).ToString())[4]);



            if (curPalletCode != "ERROR" && Gripperuseful != "ScanerNG" && Gripperuseful != "CammerNG")
            {
                product_result_xyu_add.PositionIndex = Convert.ToString(traycammerposition + 1);
                product_result_xyu_add.Result = CCDAlogrithmStatus;
                product_result_xyu_add.X = SysPara.visionposition.X;
                product_result_xyu_add.Y = SysPara.visionposition.Y;
                product_result_xyu_add.U = SysPara.visionposition.U;

                SysPara.Product_Result_XYU.Enqueue(product_result_xyu_add);
            }
            if (curPalletCode == "ERROR" || Gripperuseful == "ScanerNG" || Gripperuseful == "CammerNG")
            {
                product_result_xyu_add.PositionIndex = Convert.ToString(traycammerposition + 1);
                product_result_xyu_add.Result = false;
                product_result_xyu_add.X = "000.000";
                product_result_xyu_add.Y = "000.000";
                product_result_xyu_add.U = "000.000";

                SysPara.Product_Result_XYU.Enqueue(product_result_xyu_add);
            }

            traycammerposition++;

            QRma++;

            int productCount = 0;

            if (((Int16)GetRecipeValue("RSet", "PalletAssembCount") - SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count) / 3 != 0)
            {
                productCount = 3;
            }
            else
            {
                productCount = ((Int16)GetRecipeValue("RSet", "PalletAssembCount") - SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count);
            }

            if (QRma >= productCount)
            {
                QRma = 0;
            }

            if (SysPara.Product_Result_XYU.Count == productCount)
            {
                OB_RobotLight.Off();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }

            return FCResultType.CASE1;
        }

        //R1 Goto tray assemb
        private FCResultType flowChart37_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            Product_Result_XYU product_result_xyu_add = new Product_Result_XYU();
            string x = "000.000";
            string y = "000.000";
            string u = "000.000";

            if (SysPara.Product_Result_XYU.Count > 0)
            {
                product_result_xyu_add = SysPara.Product_Result_XYU.ToList()[(InvertOrder(SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count)) - 1];

                if (Convert.ToDouble(product_result_xyu_add.X) < 0)
                {
                    if ((Math.Abs(Convert.ToDouble(product_result_xyu_add.X)) - 100.0) >= 0)
                        x = Convert.ToDouble(product_result_xyu_add.X).ToString("000.00");
                    else
                        x = Convert.ToDouble(product_result_xyu_add.X).ToString("00.000");
                }
                else { x = Convert.ToDouble(product_result_xyu_add.X).ToString("000.000"); }

                if (Convert.ToDouble(product_result_xyu_add.Y) < 0)
                {
                    if ((Math.Abs(Convert.ToDouble(product_result_xyu_add.Y)) - 100.0) >= 0)
                        y = Convert.ToDouble(product_result_xyu_add.Y).ToString("000.00");
                    else
                        y = Convert.ToDouble(product_result_xyu_add.Y).ToString("00.000");
                }
                else { y = Convert.ToDouble(product_result_xyu_add.Y).ToString("000.000"); }

                if (Convert.ToDouble(product_result_xyu_add.U) < 0)
                {
                    if ((Math.Abs(Convert.ToDouble(product_result_xyu_add.U)) - 100.0) >= 0)
                        u = Convert.ToDouble(product_result_xyu_add.U).ToString("000.00");
                    else
                        u = Convert.ToDouble(product_result_xyu_add.U).ToString("00.000");
                }
                else { u = Convert.ToDouble(product_result_xyu_add.U).ToString("000.000"); }
            }

            string[] pos = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonPosition, "Discharge for three");

            // 【修改点】位置号从 1~12 改为 1~4
            int groupPosition = (SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count / 3) + 1;

            MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "Command：" + pos[2]
                + "Pos：" + string.Format("{0:d3}", groupPosition)
                + "AppIndex：" + InvertOrder(SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count), SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);

            if (Robot.WriteToEpson(pos[2], string.Format("{0:d3}", groupPosition),
                string.Format("{0:d2}", InvertOrder(SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count)), 20000, x, y, u))
            {
                RunTM.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }

        //Wait R1 assemb compelet
        private FCResultType flowChart39_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            int ret = Robot.ReadFromEpson();
            if (ret == 1)
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot to put product position ok", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                RunTM.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else if (ret == 0)
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonErrInfo, Robot.ErorrCode)[1], SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);

                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            else if (RunTM.On(SysPara.TimeOutSec)) { MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot to put product position timeout", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog); SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1635"); return FCResultType.IDLE; }        //R1 timeout
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }

        //Judge assemb count=12
        private FCResultType flowChart41_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            FeederT.Restart();
            LastProducts = false;
            if (SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count > 0)
            {
                if (SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count >= 12 && bProductAllNG)
                {
                    // ... 原有全NG处理逻辑完全保持不变 ...
                    SDKKernal.ShowAlarm("0021");
                    reintento.Dispose();
                    reintento = new Reintento();
                    reintento.fnSetTextMessageNShow("Please confirm wheter there is a product on the tray", true, false, true);
                    if (reintento.dResult == DialogResult.Yes)
                    {
                        LastProducts = true;
                        traycammerposition = 0;
                        SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Clear();
                        lock (SysPara.StatusLock)
                        {
                            MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.AssembCompeletStatus = true;
                        }
                        SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Clear();
                        QRmm = 0;
                        try
                        {
                            string saveImgPath = MiddleLayer.GantryF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "SaveImagePath") + "\\";
                            for (int i = 0; i < IsHaveProductImg.Count; i++)
                            {
                                string pcbCode = PCBCodePallet[i * 2];
                                if (pcbCode == null || pcbCode == "") pcbCode = "NA";
                                string imageName = string.Format(@"{0}_{1}_NA_NA_{2}_{3}.bmp",
                                                  pcbCode,
                                                     scanPellatCode[i * 2],
                                                      IsHaveProductImg[i].FeederTimer,
                                                     MiddleLayer.MesF2.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EQPID"));
                                using (CogImageFileBMP img1 = new CogImageFileBMP())
                                {
                                    if (!Directory.Exists(saveImgPath)) { Directory.CreateDirectory(saveImgPath); }
                                    Image newimg = IsHaveProductImg[i].FeederImage;
                                    newimg.Save(saveImgPath + imageName, System.Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat.Bmp);
                                    newimg.Dispose();
                                }
                            }
                            IsHaveProductImg.Clear();
                        }
                        catch (Exception) { }
                        return FCResultType.CASE3;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }
                }

                int palletAssembCount = (Int32)GetRecipeValue("RSet", "PalletAssembCount");

                // 【修改点】一次处理3个产品
                if (SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count < palletAssembCount)
                {
                    int remainingSlots = palletAssembCount - SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count;
                    int productsToAssemble = Math.Min(3, remainingSlots);
                    productsToAssemble = Math.Min(productsToAssemble, SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count);

                    // 先取出 productsToAssemble 个产品的Code
                    List<string> productCodes = new List<string>();
                    for (int i = 0; i < productsToAssemble; i++)
                    {
                        productCodes.Add(SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Dequeue().Code);
                    }

                    // 批量处理每个产品的数据
                    for (int idx = 0; idx < productsToAssemble; idx++)
                    {
                        if (idx == 0)
                        {
                            curPalletCode = SysPara.PalletCode1;
                        }
                        else if (idx == 1)
                        {
                            curPalletCode = SysPara.PalletCode2;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            curPalletCode = SysPara.PalletCode3;
                        }
                        string productCode = productCodes[idx];
                        bool isOK = (throwingjudge == false);
                        string resultStatus = isOK ? "OK" : "NG";
                        bool pressureResult = isOK;

                        SysPara.ProductPalletCode = new Product_Pallet_Code();
                        SysPara.ProductPalletCode.ProdcutCode = productCode;
                        SysPara.ProductPalletCode.PalletCode = curPalletCode;
                        SysPara.ProductPalletCode.PressureResult = pressureResult;
                        SysPara.ProductPalletCode.productscanresult = productCode == "ScanNG" ? "NG" : "OK";
                        SysPara.ProductPalletCode.productcammerresult = productCode == "CammerNG" ? "NG" : "OK";
                        SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Enqueue(SysPara.ProductPalletCode);

                        processData.fnAddCountUnit(isOK);

                        PreasureData1 preasureData1 = new PreasureData1();
                        preasureData1.Time = DateTime.Now.ToString("HH:mm:ss");
                        preasureData1.PalletCode = curPalletCode;
                        preasureData1.ProductCode = productCode;
                        preasureData1.ScanResult = productCode == "ScanerNG" ? "NG" : "OK";
                        preasureData1.CammerResult = productCode == "CammerNG" ? "NG" : "OK";
                        preasureData1.Result = resultStatus;

                        if (!MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "IsDryCycleMode") || MiddleLayer.ProcessF.bMESEnabled)
                        {
                            WriteMesByOne(curPalletCode, SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count, isOK, isOK ? "Pass" : "Fail");
                        }

                        processData.fnUpdateResult1(preasureData1, CurrentDisplayCount);
                        CurrentDisplayCount++;

                        MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production,
                            "assemble data :" + " " + Convert.ToString(SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count) + ", result: " + preasureData1.Result + ", pallet code: " + curPalletCode + ", product code: " + productCode,
                            SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                    }

                    if (throwingjudge == true)
                    {
                        throwingjudge = false;
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    // 料盘已满，处理多余产品
                    for (int i = 0; i < 3 && SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count > 0; i++)
                    {
                        string productCode = SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Dequeue().Code;
                        if (i == 0)
                        {
                            curPalletCode = SysPara.PalletCode1;
                        }
                        else if (i == 1)
                        {
                            curPalletCode = SysPara.PalletCode2;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            curPalletCode = SysPara.PalletCode3;
                        }
                        SysPara.ProductPalletCode = new Product_Pallet_Code();
                        SysPara.ProductPalletCode.ProdcutCode = productCode;
                        SysPara.ProductPalletCode.PalletCode = curPalletCode;
                        SysPara.ProductPalletCode.PressureResult = false;
                        SysPara.ProductPalletCode.productscanresult = productCode == "ScanNG" ? "NG" : "OK";
                        SysPara.ProductPalletCode.productcammerresult = productCode == "CammerNG" ? "NG" : "OK";
                        SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Enqueue(SysPara.ProductPalletCode);

                        PreasureData1 preasureData1 = new PreasureData1();
                        preasureData1.Time = DateTime.Now.ToString("HH:mm:ss");
                        preasureData1.PalletCode = curPalletCode;
                        preasureData1.ProductCode = productCode;
                        preasureData1.ScanResult = productCode == "ScanerNG" ? "NG" : "OK";
                        preasureData1.CammerResult = productCode == "CammerNG" ? "NG" : "OK";
                        preasureData1.Result = "N/A";

                        if (!MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "IsDryCycleMode") || MiddleLayer.ProcessF.bMESEnabled)
                        {
                            WriteMesByOne(curPalletCode, SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count, false, "Fail");
                        }

                        processData.fnUpdateResult1(preasureData1, CurrentDisplayCount);
                        CurrentDisplayCount++;

                        MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production,
                            "assemble data :" + " " + Convert.ToString(SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count) + ", result: N/A, pallet code: " + curPalletCode + ", product code: " + productCode,
                            SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                    }
                    throwingjudge = false;
                }

                // ... 后面的代码（maxrows、满盘判断等）完全保持不变 ...
                int maxrows = Convert.ToInt32(MiddleLayer.GantryF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "MaxDisplay"));
                if (maxrows <= 0) maxrows = 12;
                if (processData.fnRowsCount() >= maxrows) processData.fnRemoveLastResult();

                if (SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count == (Int16)GetRecipeValue("RSet", "PalletAssembCount"))
                {
                    LastProducts = true;
                    MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot finish assemb product 12", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                    traycammerposition = 0;
                    SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Clear();
                    lock (SysPara.StatusLock)
                    {
                        MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.AssembCompeletStatus = true;
                    }
                    SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Clear();
                    QRmm = 0;
                    SysPara.Product_Result_XYU.Clear();
                    processData.UpdatePalletCount();
                    if (MiddleLayer.SystemF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EnablePalletCount"))
                    {
                        int index = GetSettingValue("MSet", "ProQuantity");
                    }
                    if (GetRecipeValue("RSet", "FeederPickIndex") % 3 != 0)
                    {
                        DataTable dt = RecipeData.Tables["RSet"];
                        dt.Rows[0]["FeederPickIndex"] = ((GetRecipeValue("RSet", "FeederPickIndex") / 3) + 1) * 3;
                        dt.AcceptChanges();
                        this.WriteRecipeData(string.Format("{0}\\{1}.xml", SysPara.RecipeDataDirectory, SysPara.RecipeName));
                    }
                    try
                    {
                        string saveImgPath = MiddleLayer.GantryF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "SaveImagePath") + "\\";
                        for (int i = 0; i < IsHaveProductImg.Count; i++)
                        {
                            string pcbCode = PCBCodePallet[i * 2];
                            if (pcbCode == null || pcbCode == "") pcbCode = "NA";
                            string imageName = string.Format(@"{0}_{1}_NA_NA_{2}_{3}.bmp",
                                              pcbCode,
                                                 scanPellatCode[i * 2],
                                                  IsHaveProductImg[i].FeederTimer,
                                                 MiddleLayer.MesF2.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EQPID"));
                            using (CogImageFileBMP img1 = new CogImageFileBMP())
                            {
                                if (!Directory.Exists(saveImgPath)) { Directory.CreateDirectory(saveImgPath); }
                                Image newimg = IsHaveProductImg[i].FeederImage;
                                newimg.Save(saveImgPath + imageName, System.Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat.Bmp);
                                newimg.Dispose();
                            }
                        }
                        IsHaveProductImg.Clear();
                    }
                    catch (Exception) { }

                    if (GetSettingValue("MSet", "EPlaningCounts"))
                    {
                        int Planing = GetSettingValue("MSet", "ProQuantity");
                        int finished = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "finishedCounts");
                        if (Planing == finished)
                        {
                            traycammerposition = 0;
                            SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Clear();
                            lock (SysPara.StatusLock)
                            {
                                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.AssembCompeletStatus = true;
                            }
                            SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Clear();
                            QRmm = 0;
                            SysPara.Product_Result_XYU.Clear();
                            processData.UpdatePalletCount();
                            DataTable dt = RecipeData.Tables["RSet"];
                            dt.Rows[0]["FeederPickIndex"] = 0;
                            dt.AcceptChanges();
                            this.WriteRecipeData(string.Format("{0}\\{1}.xml", SysPara.RecipeDataDirectory, SysPara.RecipeName));
                            return FCResultType.NEXT;
                        }
                    }
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
                else if (SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count > 0)
                {
                    QRmm++;
                    return FCResultType.CASE1;
                }
                else
                {
                    MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot finish assemb product 3 ", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                    QRmm = 0;
                    SysPara.Product_Result_XYU.Clear();
                    if (GetSettingValue("MSet", "EPlaningCounts"))
                    {
                        int Planing = GetSettingValue("MSet", "ProQuantity");
                        int finished = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "finishedCounts");
                        if (Planing == finished)
                        {
                            traycammerposition = 0;
                            SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Clear();
                            lock (SysPara.StatusLock)
                            {
                                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.AssembCompeletStatus = true;
                            }
                            SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Clear();
                            QRmm = 0;
                            SysPara.Product_Result_XYU.Clear();
                            processData.UpdatePalletCount();
                            DataTable dt = RecipeData.Tables["RSet"];
                            dt.Rows[0]["FeederPickIndex"] = 0;
                            dt.AcceptChanges();
                            this.WriteRecipeData(string.Format("{0}\\{1}.xml", SysPara.RecipeDataDirectory, SysPara.RecipeName));
                            try
                            {
                                string saveImgPath = MiddleLayer.GantryF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "SaveImagePath") + "\\";
                                for (int i = 0; i < IsHaveProductImg.Count; i++)
                                {
                                    string pcbCode = PCBCodePallet[i * 2];
                                    if (pcbCode == null || pcbCode == "") pcbCode = "NA";
                                    string imageName = string.Format(@"{0}_{1}_NA_NA_{2}_{3}.bmp",
                                                      pcbCode,
                                                         scanPellatCode[i * 2],
                                                          IsHaveProductImg[i].FeederTimer,
                                                         MiddleLayer.MesF2.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EQPID"));
                                    using (CogImageFileBMP img1 = new CogImageFileBMP())
                                    {
                                        if (!Directory.Exists(saveImgPath)) { Directory.CreateDirectory(saveImgPath); }
                                        Image newimg = IsHaveProductImg[i].FeederImage;
                                        newimg.Save(saveImgPath + imageName, System.Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat.Bmp);
                                        newimg.Dispose();
                                    }
                                }
                                IsHaveProductImg.Clear();
                            }
                            catch (Exception) { }
                            return FCResultType.NEXT;
                        }
                    }
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
            }
            else
            {
                QRmm = 0;
                SysPara.Product_Result_XYU.Clear();
                if (GetSettingValue("MSet", "EPlaningCounts"))
                {
                    int Planing = GetSettingValue("MSet", "ProQuantity");
                    int finished = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "finishedCounts");
                    if (Planing == finished)
                    {
                        traycammerposition = 0;
                        SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Clear();
                        lock (SysPara.StatusLock)
                        {
                            MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.AssembCompeletStatus = true;
                        }
                        SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Clear();
                        QRmm = 0;
                        SysPara.Product_Result_XYU.Clear();
                        processData.UpdatePalletCount();
                        DataTable dt = RecipeData.Tables["RSet"];
                        dt.Rows[0]["FeederPickIndex"] = 0;
                        dt.AcceptChanges();
                        this.WriteRecipeData(string.Format("{0}\\{1}.xml", SysPara.RecipeDataDirectory, SysPara.RecipeName));
                        try
                        {
                            string saveImgPath = MiddleLayer.GantryF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "SaveImagePath") + "\\";
                            for (int i = 0; i < IsHaveProductImg.Count; i++)
                            {
                                string pcbCode = PCBCodePallet[i * 2];
                                if (pcbCode == null || pcbCode == "") pcbCode = "NA";
                                string imageName = string.Format(@"{0}_{1}_NA_NA_{2}_{3}.bmp",
                                                  pcbCode,
                                                     scanPellatCode[i * 2],
                                                      IsHaveProductImg[i].FeederTimer,
                                                     MiddleLayer.MesF2.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EQPID"));
                                using (CogImageFileBMP img1 = new CogImageFileBMP())
                                {
                                    if (!Directory.Exists(saveImgPath)) { Directory.CreateDirectory(saveImgPath); }
                                    Image newimg = IsHaveProductImg[i].FeederImage;
                                    newimg.Save(saveImgPath + imageName, System.Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat.Bmp);
                                    newimg.Dispose();
                                }
                            }
                            IsHaveProductImg.Clear();
                        }
                        catch (Exception) { }
                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    }
                }
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart45_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            //if(SysPara.NGpositionCount==1&& InvertOrder(SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count)==3)
            //{
            //    if (!GetRecipeValue("RSet", "NGbuffer2"))
            //    {
            //        SysPara.NGpositionCount = 2;
            //    }
            //    else if (!GetRecipeValue("RSet", "NGbuffer3"))
            //    {
            //        SysPara.NGpositionCount = 3;
            //    }
            //    else
            //    {
            //        DataTable dt = RecipeData.Tables["RSet"];
            //        dt.Rows[0]["NGbuffer1"] = true;
            //        dt.AcceptChanges();
            //        this.WriteRecipeData(string.Format("{0}\\{1}.xml", SysPara.RecipeDataDirectory, SysPara.RecipeName));
            //        return FCResultType.CASE1;
            //    }
            //}

            int GripperIndex = InvertOrder(SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count);

            if (Robot.WriteToEpson("016", string.Format("{0:d3}", SysPara.NGpositionCount), string.Format("{0:d2}", GripperIndex), 20000))
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot to throwing position", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                RunTM.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart40_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            int ret = Robot.ReadFromEpson();
            if (ret == 1) { MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot to throwing position ok", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog); return FCResultType.NEXT; }
            else if (ret == 0)
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonErrInfo, Robot.ErorrCode)[1], SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            else if (RunTM.On(SysPara.TimeOutSec)) { MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot to throwing position timeout", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog); SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1635"); return FCResultType.IDLE; }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart70_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            string NGbufferIndex = "NGbuffer1";
            switch (SysPara.NGpositionCount)
            {
                case 1:
                    NGbufferIndex = "NGbuffer1";
                    break;
                case 2:
                    NGbufferIndex = "NGbuffer2";
                    break;
                case 3:
                    NGbufferIndex = "NGbuffer3";
                    break;
            }
            DataTable dt = RecipeData.Tables["RSet"];
            dt.Rows[0][NGbufferIndex] = true;
            dt.AcceptChanges();
            this.WriteRecipeData(string.Format("{0}\\{1}.xml", SysPara.RecipeDataDirectory, SysPara.RecipeName));
            //if(Convert.ToBoolean(dt.Rows[0]["NGbuffer1"]) == true
            //    && Convert.ToBoolean(dt.Rows[0]["NGbuffer2"]) == true
            //    && Convert.ToBoolean(dt.Rows[0]["NGbuffer3"]) == true)
            //{
            //    MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "ng had full", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
            //    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("12005");
            //}
            throwingjudge = true;
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        //Scan NG
        private FCResultType flowChart68_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Clear();
            SysPara.Product_Result_XYU.Clear();
            curR2HaveNum1 = 0;
            CCDReuslt_EachLoop.Clear();

            SysPara.ScanCount = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "FeederPickIndex");//LFF-0209   SysPara.FeederProductCount
            if (SysPara.ScanCount % 3 != 0)
            {
                SysPara.ScanCount = SysPara.ScanCount + 3 - SysPara.ScanCount % 3;
            }
            if (SysPara.ScanCount >= (Int32)GetRecipeValue("RSet", "CoverAssembCount"))
            {
                SysPara.JAGStarytEvent.Set();
                SysPara.ScanCount = 0;
                FeederT.Restart();
            }
            //SysPara.ScanCount++;
            DataTable dt = RecipeData.Tables["RSet"];
            dt.Rows[0]["FeederPickIndex"] = SysPara.ScanCount;
            dt.AcceptChanges();
            this.WriteRecipeData(string.Format("{0}\\{1}.xml", SysPara.RecipeDataDirectory, SysPara.RecipeName));
            SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Clear();

            if (SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Count >= 3)
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "scan product had full 3 and has ng", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                return FCResultType.CASE1;
            }
            else
            {
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
        }

        private void btnTeachPickVisPos_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            H1_PickupPos.RunTB();
            DataTable dt = RecipeData.Tables["Rset"];
            if (H1_PickupPos.IsAccept)
            {
                dt.Rows[0]["PickTeachVisX"] = H1_PickupPos.GetOutput("X").ToString();
                dt.Rows[0]["PickTeachVisY"] = H1_PickupPos.GetOutput("Y").ToString();
                dt.Rows[0]["PickTeachVisU"] = H1_PickupPos.GetOutput("Angle").ToString();
                dt.AcceptChanges();
            }
            else
            {
                dt.Rows[0]["PickTeachVisX"] = Convert.ToString(999.999);
                dt.Rows[0]["PickTeachVisY"] = Convert.ToString(999.999);
                dt.Rows[0]["PickTeachVisU"] = Convert.ToString(999.999);
                dt.AcceptChanges();
            }
        }
        private void btnTeachPickRobotPos_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            DataTable dt = RecipeData.Tables["Rset"];
            double? x, y, u;
            //if (RobotZebra.ReadPR(20, ref ReadRobotPR)) 
            if (Robot.ConnectStatus)
            {
                Robot.WriteToEpson("012", "005", "FF", 2000, "FFFFFFF", "FFFFFFF", "FFFFFFF");
                DelayMs(2000);
                double[] p = Robot.ReadPositionFromEpson();
                DelayMs(500);
                p = Robot.ReadPositionFromEpson();
                x = p[0]; y = p[1]; u = p[2];
                RobotPose = p;

                //double[] p = new double[3];
                //p[0] = 622.644;
                //p[1] = 162.369;
                //p[2] = 177.888;
                //x = p[0]; y = p[1]; u = p[2];
                //RobotPose = p;
            }
            else
            {
                x = null;
                y = null;
                u = null;
            }
            dt.Rows[0]["PickTeachRobotX"] = x.ToString();
            dt.Rows[0]["PickTeachRobotY"] = y.ToString();
            dt.Rows[0]["PickTeachRobotU"] = u.ToString();
            //dt.AcceptChanges(); u.ToString();
            dt.AcceptChanges();
        }

        private void btnTeachPickRobotPos2_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            DataTable dt = RecipeData.Tables["Rset"];

            if (RobotZebra.ReadPR(20, ref ReadRobotPR)) //PR20
            {
                x = ReadRobotPR[20, 0];
                y = ReadRobotPR[20, 1];
                u = ReadRobotPR[20, 5];
            }
            else
            {
                x = null;
                y = null;
                u = null;
            }
            dt.Rows[0]["PickTeachRobotX2"] = x;
            dt.Rows[0]["PickTeachRobotY2"] = y;
            dt.Rows[0]["PickTeachRobotU2"] = u;
            dt.AcceptChanges();
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart52_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            OB_RobotLight.On();
            DelayMs(500);

            CogImage8Grey img = null;
            Cog3DVect3 xyu = new Cog3DVect3();

            if (H1_PickupPos.TakePicture())
            {
                img = H1_PickupPos.InputImage;
                if (Robot.ConnectStatus)
                {
                    //if (Robot.WriteToEpson("015", "000", "FF", 2000, "FFFFFFF", "FFFFFFF", "FFFFFFF"))
                    // {
                    double[] Pos = new double[3];
                    Pos = RobotPose;
                    xyu = new Cog3DVect3(Pos[0], Pos[1], Pos[2]);
                    //   }
                    //  else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
                }
                else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }

                Tuple<Cog3DVect3, CogImage8Grey> xyu_Image = new Tuple<Cog3DVect3, CogImage8Grey>(xyu, img);
                visCalibData.Add(xyu_Image);
                CalibPictureCount++;
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart54_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            System.Threading.Tasks.Task.Factory.StartNew(() =>
            {
                if (!H1_PickupPos.TB.Inputs.Contains("CalibDatas"))
                {
                    H1_PickupPos.TB.Inputs.Add(new Cognex.VisionPro.ToolBlock.CogToolBlockTerminal("CalibDatas", new List<Tuple<Cog3DVect3, CogImage8Grey>>()));
                }
                H1_PickupPos.visCalibData = visCalibData;
                H1_PickupPos.TB.Inputs["ISCalib"].Value = true;
                H1_PickupPos.TB.Inputs["GrabPosIndex"].Value = 0;
                H1_PickupPos.RunTB();
                H1_PickupPos.TB.Inputs["ISCalib"].Value = false;
                OB_RobotLight.Off();
                DataTable dt = RecipeData.Tables["R1_PickVisCalib"];
                List<Tuple<Cog3DVect2, Cog3DVect2>> visRobotData = new List<Tuple<Cog3DVect2, Cog3DVect2>>();
                visRobotData = H1_PickupPos.GetOutput("VisRobotData") as List<Tuple<Cog3DVect2, Cog3DVect2>>;
                if (H1_PickupPos.IsAccept)
                {
                    dt.Clear();

                    for (int i = 0; i < visRobotData.Count; i++)
                    {
                        DataRow row = dt.NewRow();
                        row["Calib_Index"] = (i + 1).ToString();
                        row["Calib_PixelX"] = visRobotData[i].Item1.X.ToString("f3");
                        row["Calib_PixelY"] = visRobotData[i].Item1.Y.ToString("f3");
                        row["Calib_RobotX"] = visRobotData[i].Item2.X.ToString("f3");
                        row["Calib_RobotY"] = visRobotData[i].Item2.Y.ToString("f3");
                        dt.Rows.Add(row);
                    }
                    dt.AcceptChanges();
                }
                else
                {
                    dt.Rows[0]["Calib_Index"] = "0";
                    dt.Rows[0]["Calib_PixelX"] = "999";
                    dt.Rows[0]["Calib_PixelY"] = "999";
                    dt.Rows[0]["Calib_RobotX"] = "999";
                    dt.Rows[0]["Calib_RobotY"] = "999";
                    dt.AcceptChanges();
                }
            });

            bAutoCalibFlow = true;
            return FCResultType.IDLE;
        }

        public void BConnect()
        {
            bConnect = PFClient.Connect();
            bComStart = PFClient.StartCommunication();
            bSubscribe = PFClient.SubscribeLastTighteningResult();
            PFKeepAlive();
            PFClient.CommunicationAlive();
        }

        public void BConnect2()
        {
            bConnect2 = PFClient2.Connect();
            bComStart2 = PFClient2.StartCommunication();
            bSubscribe2 = PFClient2.SubscribeLastTighteningResult();
            PFKeepAlive2();
            PFClient2.CommunicationAlive();
        }

        {

                sData += MiddleLayer.GantryF.DataTotal[i] + "\r\n";
            }
            MiddleLayer.GantryF.TPFP = true;
            //SaveFile.SaveMES(MiddleLayer.GantryF.textBox23.Text, sData, MiddleLayer.GantryF.TPFP, sTtime);
        }

        private void button5_Click_1(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            string[] pos1 = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonPosition, "GetPosition");
            if (pos1 != null)
            {
                if (Robot.WriteToEpson(pos1[2], pos1[3], pos1[4], 30000))
                {
                    int ret = Robot.ReadFromEpson();
                    if (ret == 12)
                    {
                        txtReadPRX.Text = Robot.ReadRobotXYU[0].ToString();
                        txtReadPRY.Text = Robot.ReadRobotXYU[1].ToString();
                        txtReadPRZ.Text = Robot.ReadRobotXYU[2].ToString();
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    txtReadPRX.Text = "-999.999";
                    txtReadPRY.Text = "-999.999";
                    txtReadPRZ.Text = "-999.999";
                }
            }
        }

        private void bt_ReadIO_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            bt_ReadIO.Enabled = false;
            this.flowChart1.TaskReset();
            bReadIOFlow = false;
            System.Threading.Tasks.Task.Factory.StartNew(() =>
            {
                while (!bReadIOFlow)
                {
                    flowChart1.TaskRun();
                }
            });
        }

        private void bt_WriteIO_Click_1(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            WriteIOTimer.Restart();
            bool ret;
            bt_WriteIO.Enabled = false;
            System.Threading.Tasks.Task.Factory.StartNew(() =>
            {
                while (true)
                {
                    ret = GetRobotDO();
                    if (ret || WriteIOTimer.On(5000)) { bt_WriteIO.Enabled = true; break; }
                }
            });
        }
        #region readIOFlow
        private FCResultType flowChart1_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            ReadIOTimer.Restart();
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart2_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            string[] pos1 = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonPosition, "Read RobotDI");
            if (ReadIOTimer.On(5000))
            {
                ReadIOTimer.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            if (Robot.WriteToEpson(pos1[2], pos1[3], pos1[4], 30000))
            {
                ReadIOTimer.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart3_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            int ret = Robot.ReadFromEpson();
            if (ReadIOTimer.On(5000))
            {
                bReadIOFlow = true;
                bt_ReadIO.Enabled = true;
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            if (ret == 103)
            {
                ReadIO = Robot.Input;
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }

            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart4_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            string[] pos1 = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonPosition, "Read RobotDO");
            if (ReadIOTimer.On(5000))
            {
                ReadIOTimer.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            if (Robot.WriteToEpson(pos1[2], pos1[3], pos1[4], 30000))
            {
                ReadIOTimer.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }



        private FCResultType flowChart5_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            int ret = Robot.ReadFromEpson();
            if (ReadIOTimer.On(5000))
            {
                bReadIOFlow = true;
                bt_ReadIO.Enabled = true;
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            if (ret == 102)
            {
                WriteIO = Robot.Output;
                bReadIOFlow = true;
                bt_ReadIO.Enabled = true;
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }

            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }
        #endregion

        public string[] GetDataByAnnotation(VUControl.VUDatagridView dgv, string annotation)
        {
            string[] GetDataInfo = dgv.GetDataArrayByFlagName(1, annotation);
            return GetDataInfo;
        }

        public bool ConnectEpsonRobot()
        {
            if (Robot.ConnectToEpson(MiddleLayer.GantryF.GetRecipeValue("RSet", "RobotPoint1"), MiddleLayer.GantryF.GetRecipeValue("RSet", "RobotPort")))
            {
                if (!bgwEpsonCommunicate.IsBusy)
                    bgwEpsonCommunicate.RunWorkerAsync();
                return true;
            }
            else
            {
                return false;
            }
        }


        private void chk_Output_1_CheckedChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (chk_Output_1.Checked)
            {
                chk_Output_1.BackgroundImage = Properties.Resources.Green;
                WriteIO[0] = "1";
            }
            else
            {
                chk_Output_1.BackgroundImage = Properties.Resources.Red;
                WriteIO[0] = "0";
            }
        }

        private void label114_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (MiddleLayer.GantryF.Robot.ConnectStatus)
            {
                string[] pos = MiddleLayer.GantryF.GetDataByAnnotation(MiddleLayer.GantryF.dgv_EpsonPosition, "Read Robot Speed");
                if (MiddleLayer.GantryF.Robot.WriteToEpson(pos[2], string.Format("{0:d3}", SysPara.ScanCount), pos[4], 30000))
                {
                    Thread.Sleep(50);
                    int ret = MiddleLayer.GantryF.Robot.ReadFromEpson();
                    if (ret == 105)
                    {
                        trackBar1.Value = Convert.ToInt32(string.Format("{0:d2}", MiddleLayer.GantryF.Robot.Speed));
                        label115.Text = Convert.ToString(trackBar1.Value);
                    }
                    else if (ret == -105)
                    {
                        MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "read robot speed fail", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                    }
                }
                else { MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "read robot speed fail", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog); }
            }
        }

        private void Mouse_UP(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
        {
            if (MiddleLayer.GantryF.Robot.ConnectStatus)
            {
                string[] pos = MiddleLayer.GantryF.GetDataByAnnotation(MiddleLayer.GantryF.dgv_EpsonPosition, "Write Robot Speed");
                MiddleLayer.GantryF.Robot.SetSpeed(pos[2], pos[3], pos[4], 30000, string.Format("{0:d7}", Convert.ToInt16(trackBar1.Value)));
            }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart69_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (
                MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "IsDryCycleMode")
                )
            {
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }

            if (
                !MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "IsDryCycleMode")
                )
            {
                if (MiddleLayer.SystemF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "DisableFeeder"))
                {
                    return FCResultType.IDLE;
                }
                else
                {
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
            }
            return FCResultType.IDLE;
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart72_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            if (IB_FeederReady.Off())
            {
                //SysPara.NPShowAlarm("15012");
                reintento.Dispose();
                reintento = new Reintento();
                reintento.fnSetTextMessageNShow("confrim feeder whether is ready,yes or no", true, false, true);
                if (reintento.dResult == DialogResult.Yes)
                {
                    OB_FeederStart.On();
                    FeederTM.Restart();
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
                else
                {
                    return FCResultType.IDLE;
                }
            }
            //SysPara.NPShowAlarm("13005");
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart73_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (FeederTM.On(40000))
            {

                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1101");
                reintento.Dispose();
                reintento = new Reintento();
                reintento.fnSetTextMessageNShow("confrim feeder whether is ready,yes or no", true, false, true);
                if (reintento.dResult == DialogResult.Yes)
                {
                }
                else
                {
                    return FCResultType.IDLE;
                }
            }
            if (IB_FeederInPlace.On())
            {
                SysPara.JAGStarytEvent.Reset();
                OB_FeederStart.Off();
                OB_FeederCompleted.Off();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            return FCResultType.IDLE;
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart74_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (SysPara.JAGStarytEvent.WaitOne(0))
            {
                if (IB_FeederAlarm.On())
                {

                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1100");
                    return FCResultType.CASE1;
                }
                if (IB_FeederLack.On())
                {

                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1104");
                    return FCResultType.CASE1;
                }
                if (!IB_FeederReady.On())
                {

                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1101");
                    return FCResultType.CASE1;
                }
                else
                {
                    MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot had take all product", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                    SysPara.JAGStarytEvent.Reset();
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
            }
            if (IB_FeederAlarm.On())
            {

                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1100");
            }
            if (IB_FeederLack.On())
            {

                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1104");
            }
            if (!IB_FeederReady.On())
            {

                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1101");
            }
            return FCResultType.IDLE;
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart82_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (!GetRecipeValue("RSet", "NGbuffer1"))
            {
                SysPara.NGpositionCount = 1;
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else if (!GetRecipeValue("RSet", "NGbuffer2"))
            {
                SysPara.NGpositionCount = 2;
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else if (!GetRecipeValue("RSet", "NGbuffer3"))
            {
                SysPara.NGpositionCount = 3;
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {

                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "NG had full", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("0006");
                reintento.fnSetTextMessageNShow("NG tray is full!!! sure has been replaced?", true, false, true);
                if (reintento.dResult == DialogResult.Yes)
                {
                    DataTable dt = RecipeData.Tables["RSet"];
                    dt.Rows[0]["NGbuffer1"] = false;
                    dt.Rows[0]["NGbuffer2"] = false;
                    dt.Rows[0]["NGbuffer3"] = false;
                    dt.AcceptChanges();
                    SDKKernal.ClearAlarm("0006");
                    this.WriteRecipeData(string.Format("{0}\\{1}.xml", SysPara.RecipeDataDirectory, SysPara.RecipeName));
                    SysPara.NGpositionCount = 1;
                    NGfullcheck = true;
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
                else
                {
                    DelayMs(500);
                    return FCResultType.IDLE;
                }
            }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart85_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            int ret = Robot.ReadFromEpson();
            if (ret == 1) { MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot to throwing  cammer position ok", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog); return FCResultType.NEXT; }
            else if (ret == 0)
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonErrInfo, Robot.ErorrCode)[1], SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            else if (RunTM.On(SysPara.TimeOutSec)) { MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot to throwing  cammer position timeout", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog); SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1635"); return FCResultType.IDLE; }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart1_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            if (MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "IsDryCycleMode"))
            {
                DataTable dt = RecipeData.Tables["RSet"];
                dt.Rows[0]["FeederPickIndex"] = Convert.ToInt16(0);
                dt.AcceptChanges();
                this.WriteRecipeData(string.Format("{0}\\{1}.xml", SysPara.RecipeDataDirectory, SysPara.RecipeName));
            }

            else
            {
                DataTable dt = RecipeData.Tables["RSet"];
                dt.Rows[0]["FeederPickIndex"] = Convert.ToInt16(Robot.StartScanCount(GetRecipeValue("RSet", "FeederPickIndex")));
                dt.AcceptChanges();
                this.WriteRecipeData(string.Format("{0}\\{1}.xml", SysPara.RecipeDataDirectory, SysPara.RecipeName));
            }
            bInitialOk = true;
            SysPara.RIniRet = true;
            MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.uC_ResetProcessConveyorSequence.bGantryIsSafe = true;
            bRestartProcessOk = true;
            return FCResultType.IDLE;
        }

        private bool DectNetworkCable(string ip)
        {
            try
            {
                Ping ping = new Ping();
                PingReply pr = ping.Send(ip);
                if (pr.Status == IPStatus.Success)
                {
                    return true;
                }
                else
                {
                    return false;
                }
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart91_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.ToList()[0].Code == "ScanNG" &&
                    SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.ToList()[1].Code == "ScanNG" &&
                    SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.ToList()[2].Code == "ScanNG")
            {
                //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "continue scan 3 product ng feeder replace product", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                DelayMs(200);
                SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Clear();
                if (MiddleLayer.SystemF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "continue ng 3 warming"))
                {

                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1303");
                    return FCResultType.CASE1;
                }

                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1301");
                return FCResultType.CASE1;
            }
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart92_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            bool result = false;
            for (int i = 0; i < SysPara.Product_Result_XYU.Count; i++)
            {
                result |= SysPara.Product_Result_XYU.ToList()[i].Result;

            }
            if (MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "IsDryCycleMode"))
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "Dry run finish  Check", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {
                if (CCDReuslt_EachLoop.Contains(false))
                {
                    int productCount = 0;
                    if (((Int16)GetRecipeValue("RSet", "PalletAssembCount") - SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count) / 3 != 0)
                    {
                        productCount = 3;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        productCount = ((Int16)GetRecipeValue("RSet", "PalletAssembCount") - SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count);
                    }
                    SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Clear();
                    SysPara.Product_Result_XYU.Clear();
                    curR2HaveNum1 = 0;
                    CCDReuslt_EachLoop.Clear();
                    return FCResultType.CASE1;
                }
                else
                {
                    CCDReuslt_EachLoop.Clear();
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;

                }

            }
        }

        private void button61_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            double X = 0, Y = 0, U = 0;
            int index = 0;
            string[] posArr;
            for (int i = 0; i < (int)GetSettingValue("PSet", "Row"); i++)
            {
                for (int j = 0; j < (int)GetSettingValue("PSet", "Column"); j++)
                {
                    X = GetSettingValue("PSet", "StartX") + j * GetSettingValue("PSet", "offsetX");
                    Y = GetSettingValue("PSet", "StartY") + i * GetSettingValue("PSet", "offsetY");
                    U = 0;
                    index++;
                    if (rdb_FeederCamPos.Checked)
                    {
                        posArr = new string[] { "FeederPickCamPos" + index.ToString(), X.ToString(), Y.ToString(), U.ToString(), };
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        posArr = new string[] { "ConveryAssembCamPos" + index.ToString(), X.ToString(), Y.ToString(), U.ToString(), };
                    }
                    dgv_RobotPosition.WriteRowToDataGrid(posArr);
                }
            }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart94_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (((Int16)GetRecipeValue("RSet", "PalletAssembCount") - SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count) / 3 != 0)
            {
                curProductCount = 3;
            }
            else
            {
                curProductCount = ((Int16)GetRecipeValue("RSet", "PalletAssembCount") - SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count);
            }
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart96_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            string[] pos = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonPosition, "GetPosition");
            if (Robot.WriteToEpson(pos[2], pos[3], pos[4], 30000))
            {
                RunTM.Restart();
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "Command:" + pos[2] + "Pos:" + pos[3] + "AppIndex:" + pos[4], SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart97_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            robotPos = new double[3];
            robotPos = Robot.ReadPositionFromEpson();
            if (RunTM.On(SysPara.TimeOutSec)) { SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1635"); return FCResultType.IDLE; }
            if (robotPos[0] == 0 && robotPos[1] == 0)
            {
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            else
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "Read robot xyu:" + robotPos[0].ToString() + "," + robotPos[1].ToString() + "," + robotPos[2].ToString(), SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }

        }

        DateTime Timestamp_10710 = DateTime.Now;
        JTimer Timer_10710 = new JTimer();
        private FCResultType npFlowChart9_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (SysPara.EnableMes)
            {
                List<bool> _Result_List = new List<bool>();
                List<string> _DefectCode_List = new List<string>();
                List<string> _Carrier_List = new List<string>();


                List<string> _Pocket_List = new List<string>();
                List<string> _Module_List = new List<string>();
                List<string> _LotID_List = new List<string>();

                List<string> _MaterialID_List = new List<string>();

                var keys = MESLib.CommParas.LOT_START_VALUE_LIST.Keys.ToList();
                for (int i = 0; i < keys.Count; i++)
                {
                    int key = keys[i];
                    JObject jobj = MESLib.CommParas.LOT_START_VALUE_LIST[key];

                    string lotid = jobj["LOTID"].Value<string>();
                    string pocketid = jobj["POCKETID"].Value<string>();
                    string moduleid = jobj["MODULEID"].Value<string>();
                    string materialid = MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MATERIALs[key];

                    int index = key;

                    SysPara.PalletProductCount++;// record the pallect products

                    _Pocket_List.Add(pocketid);
                    _Module_List.Add(moduleid);
                    _LotID_List.Add(lotid);

                    _Result_List.Add(true);
                    _DefectCode_List.Add("");

                    _MaterialID_List.Add(materialid);

                    _Carrier_List.Add(MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.TRAY_POSITION_QRCODEs[index]);
                }
                Timestamp_10710 = DateTime.Now;
                Timer_10710.Restart();
                MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_10710(_LotID_List, _Pocket_List, _Module_List,
                    _LotID_List, _Pocket_List, _Carrier_List, _Module_List,
                    MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PROCID, MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PRODID,
                    _Result_List, _DefectCode_List,
                    _MaterialID_List, "19", "", "");
            }

            Timer_10710.Restart();
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        DateTime Timestamp_10730 = DateTime.Now;
        JTimer Timer_10730 = new JTimer();
        private FCResultType npFlowChart6_FlowRun_1(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (SysPara.EnableMes)
            {
                List<string> _Material_List = new List<string>();
                for (int i = 0; i < MaterialID_Array.LongLength; i++)
                {
                    if (MaterialID_Array[i] != null &&
                        MaterialID_Array[i].ToLower().Trim() != "error" &&
                        MaterialID_Array[i] != "")
                    {
                        _Material_List.Add(MaterialID_Array[i]);
                    }
                }
                MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo("10730");
                MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo("Material: " + MaterialID_Array._ToString());
                Timestamp_10730 = DateTime.Now;
                MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_MULTI_MATERIALID_FAIL_OK = false;
                MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_MULTI_MATERIALID_CONFIRM_OK = false;
                MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_10730(_Material_List,
                    MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PRODID, "", "19");
                Timer_10730.Restart();
            }

            return FCResultType.NEXT;

        }
