        private FCResultType npFlowChart8_FlowRun_1(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (SysPara.EnableMes)
            {
                if (MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_ACK_WithTimeout("10730", Timestamp_10730,
                    Timer_10730, out int ACK, out string data, out bool timeout))
                {
                    if (ACK == 0)
                    {
                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70100");
                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    if (timeout)
                    {
                        // T3 Fail
                        MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_11001("2");
                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70102");
                        MiddleLayer.MainF.btnStop_Click(null, null);
                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }
                }
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        DateTime Timestamp_10701 = DateTime.Now;
        JTimer Timer_10701 = new JTimer();
        JTimer Timer_LOT_START_OUTTIME = new JTimer();
        private FCResultType npFlowChart10_FlowRun_1(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (SysPara.EnableMes)
            {
                MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_START_OK = false;
                MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_FAIL_OK = false;
                MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_START_STR = "";
                List<string> _Material_List = new List<string>();
                List<string> _Pocket_List = new List<string>();
                List<string> _ModuleID_List = new List<string>();

                int index = SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count;

                bool allempty = true;
                for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
                {
                    if (bCheckProduct[i + index])
                    {
                        allempty = false;
                        break;
                    }
                }

                if (allempty)
                {
                    return FCResultType.CASE1;
                }


                //MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.POCKETIDs = new string[] { "A1", "B1", "C1", "D1", "E1", "F1", "A2", "B2", "C2", "D2", "E2", "F2" };
                for (int i = 0; i < MaterialID_Array.LongLength; i++)
                {
                    if (MaterialID_Array[i] != null &&
                        MaterialID_Array[i].ToLower().Trim() != "error" &&
                        MaterialID_Array[i] != "")
                    {
                        if (!bCheckProduct[i + index])
                            continue;

                        _Material_List.Add(MaterialID_Array[i]);
                        _ModuleID_List.Add(MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.TRAY_POSITION_QRCODEs[index + i]);
                        _Pocket_List.Add(MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.POCKETIDs[index + i]);
                    }
                }
                Timestamp_10701 = DateTime.Now;

                // get pocketid  list and material id list
                MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_10701(_Pocket_List,
                    _ModuleID_List,
                    MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PRODID);
                Timer_LOT_START_OUTTIME.Restart();
                Timer_10701.Restart();
            }
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        DateTime Timestamp_10704 = DateTime.Now;
        JTimer Timer_10704 = new JTimer();

        private FCResultType npFlowChart11_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (SysPara.EnableMes)
            {
                try
                {
                    List<string> _Pocket_List = new List<string>();
                    List<string> _Module_List = new List<string>();
                    List<string> _LotID_List = new List<string>();
                    MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LOTIDs = new string[12];
                    PCBCode.Clear();
                    var keys = MESLib.CommParas.LOT_START_VALUE_LIST.Keys.ToList();
                    foreach (var key in keys)
                    {
                        JObject jobj = MESLib.CommParas.LOT_START_VALUE_LIST[key];

                        string lotid = jobj["LOTID"].Value<string>();
                        string pocketid = jobj["POCKETID"].Value<string>();
                        string moduleid = jobj["MODULEID"].Value<string>();
                        MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PROCID = jobj["PROCID"].Value<string>();
                        //int index = key;
                        int index = -1;
                        index = MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.GetIndex(pocketid, MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.POCKETIDs);

                        if (index == -1)
                        {
                            // Alarm
                            SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70104"); // report and RCMD not match
                            MESLib.CommParas.MesCenter.PauseRunning();
                        }
                        MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LOTIDs[index] = lotid;



                        _Pocket_List.Add(pocketid);
                        _Module_List.Add(moduleid);
                        _LotID_List.Add(lotid);
                    }
                    for (int i = 0; i < MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LOTIDs.Length; i++)
                    {
                        if (MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LOTIDs[i] == "" || MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LOTIDs[i] == null)
                        {

                        }
                        else
                            PCBCode.Add(MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LOTIDs[i]);

                    }

                    Timestamp_10704 = DateTime.Now;
                    Timer_10704.Restart();

                    MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_START_OK = false;
                    MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_FAIL_OK = false;
                    Timer_LGIT_LOT_FAIL.Restart();

                    MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_10704(_LotID_List,
                        _Pocket_List, _Module_List,
                        MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PROCID, MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PRODID);
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    MessageBox.Show("10704 Error: " + ex.Message);
                    throw new Exception("10704 Error: " + ex.Message);

                }
            }

            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart14_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (SysPara.EnableMes)
            {
                if (MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_MULTI_MATERIALID_CONFIRM_OK || MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_MULTI_MATERIALID_FAIL_OK)
                {
                    if (MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_MULTI_MATERIALID_CONFIRM_OK)
                    {    // get PROCID
                        JObject jobj = JObject.Parse(MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_MULTI_MATERIALID_CONFIRM_STR);
                        MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PROCID = jobj["EQPID"].Value<string>();
                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70110");
                        MESLib.Frms.FrmServerMessage FrmServerMessage = new MESLib.Frms.FrmServerMessage("", "Message", "", "Please check input material in MBOM", "Retry", "CLOSE", "Next Group",
                             true, true, true);
                        FrmServerMessage.fnSetTextMessageNShow();
                        if (FrmServerMessage.dResult == DialogResult.Yes)
                        {
                            return FCResultType.CASE1;
                        }
                        else if (FrmServerMessage.dResult == DialogResult.No)
                        {
                            MESLib.CommParas.MesCenter.StopRunning();
                            return FCResultType.IDLE;
                        }
                        else if (FrmServerMessage.dResult == DialogResult.Abort)
                        {
                            // 3. ng buffer
                            // looking for index
                            for (int i = 0; i < MesOK.Length; i++)
                            { MesOK[i] = false; }
                            return FCResultType.CASE2;

                        }
                    }
                }
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart15_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            if (SysPara.EnableMes)
            {
                if (MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_START_OK || MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_FAIL_OK)
                {
                    if (MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_START_OK)
                    {
                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70106");
                        ////< A7020 Type = "E" Content = "MES GET LGIT_FAIL" DoStop = "True" />
                        //return FCResultType.IDLE;
                        string[] msg = Get_LOT_FAIL_Message();
                        //DisposeFrm(FrmServerMessage);

                        FrmServerMessage FrmServerMessage = new FrmServerMessage(msg[0], msg[1]);
                        FrmServerMessage.fnSetTextMessageNShow();
                        if (FrmServerMessage.dResult == DialogResult.Yes)
                        {
                            return FCResultType.CASE1;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            MiddleLayer.MainF.btnStop_Click(null, null);
                            return FCResultType.IDLE;
                        }

                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    if (Timer_LOT_START_OUTTIME.On(60000))
                    {
                        // START OUTTIME
                        MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_11002("2");
                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("7023");
                        Timer_LOT_START_OUTTIME.Restart();
                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }
                    return FCResultType.IDLE;
                }
            }
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        public string[] Get_LOT_FAIL_Message()
        {
            string[] msg = new string[] { "", "" };
            JObject lotsinfo = MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_START_FAIL_DATA["LOTINFOLIST"].Value<JObject>();

            try
            {
                // module list
                foreach (var p in lotsinfo.Properties())
                {
                    var name = p.Name;
                    var value = JObject.Parse(p.Value.ToString());
                    msg[0] = value["MODULEID"].Value<string>();
                    break;
                }

            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                msg[0] = ex.Message;
            }
            // get text
            try
            {
                msg[1] = MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_START_FAIL_DATA["TEXT"].Value<string>();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                msg[1] = ex.Message;
            }
            return msg;
        }

        DateTime Timestamp_10723 = DateTime.Now;
        JTimer Timer_10723 = new JTimer();
        private FCResultType npFlowChart18_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (SysPara.EnableMes)
            {
                Timestamp_10723 = DateTime.Now;
                // used pocket, camera result
                bool DefectFound = false;
                for (int i = 0; i < MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PRODUCEDs.Length; i++)
                {
                    if (!SysPara.CameraCheckResult[i])
                    {
                        DefectFound = true;
                        break;
                    }
                }
                if (DefectFound)
                {
                    List<string> _LotID_List = new List<string>();
                    List<string> _PocketID_List = new List<string>();
                    List<string> _ModuleID_List = new List<string>();

                    for (int i = 0; i < MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PRODUCEDs.Length; i++)
                    {
                        if (!SysPara.CameraCheckResult[i])
                        {
                            _LotID_List.Add(MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LOTIDs[i]);
                            _PocketID_List.Add(MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.POCKETIDs[i]);
                            _ModuleID_List.Add(MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MODULEIDs[i]);
                        }
                    }
                    // defect found
                    MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_10723(_LotID_List,
                        _PocketID_List, _ModuleID_List,
                        MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PROCID,
                        MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PRODID);
                }
            }
            Timer_10723.Restart();
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart19_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (SysPara.EnableMes)
            {
                if (Timer_10723.On(100))
                {
                    if (MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_ACK("10723", Timestamp_10723, out int ACK, out string data))
                    {
                        if (ACK == 0)
                            return FCResultType.NEXT;
                        else
                        {
                            SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70100"); // ACK not 0
                            return FCResultType.IDLE;
                        }
                    }
                    Timer_10723.Restart();
                }
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        DateTime Timestamp_10401 = DateTime.Now;
        JTimer Timer_10401 = new JTimer();
        bool LastProducts = false;
        private FCResultType npFlowChart20_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (SysPara.EnableMes)
            {
                if (GetSettingValue("MSet", "EPlaningCounts"))
                {
                    int Planing = GetSettingValue("MSet", "ProQuantity");
                    int finished = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "finishedCounts");
                    if (Planing == finished)
                    {
                        LastProducts = true;
                    }
                }
                if (LastProducts)
                {
                    Timestamp_10401 = DateTime.Now;
                    Timer_10401.Restart();
                    MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_10401(((byte)MESLib.ProcessState.IDLE));
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
            }

            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart21_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (SysPara.EnableMes)
            {

                if (GetSettingValue("MSet", "EPlaningCounts"))
                {
                    int Planing = GetSettingValue("MSet", "ProQuantity");
                    int finished = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "finishedCounts");
                    if (Planing == finished)
                    {
                        LastProducts = true;
                    }
                }
                if (LastProducts)
                {
                    if (MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_ACK_WithTimeout("10401", Timestamp_10401,
                        Timer_10401, out int ACK, out string data, out bool timeout))
                    {
                        if (ACK == 0)
                        {
                            return FCResultType.NEXT;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70100");
                            return FCResultType.IDLE;
                        }
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        if (timeout)
                        {
                            // T3 Fail
                            MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_11001("2");
                            SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70102");
                            MiddleLayer.MainF.btnStop_Click(null, null);
                            return FCResultType.IDLE;
                        }
                    }
                    return FCResultType.IDLE;
                }
            }

            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart26_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            if (SysPara.EnableMes)
            {
                if (MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_START_OK || MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_FAIL_OK)
                {
                    if (MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_START_OK)
                    {
                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70106");
                        ////< A7020 Type = "E" Content = "MES GET LGIT_FAIL" DoStop = "True" />
                        //return FCResultType.IDLE;
                        string[] msg = Get_LOT_FAIL_Message();
                        //DisposeFrm(FrmServerMessage);

                        FrmServerMessage FrmServerMessage = new FrmServerMessage(msg[0], msg[1]);
                        FrmServerMessage.fnSetTextMessageNShow();
                        if (FrmServerMessage.dResult == DialogResult.Yes)
                        {
                            return FCResultType.CASE1;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            MiddleLayer.MainF.btnStop_Click(null, null);
                            return FCResultType.IDLE;
                        }
                    }
                }
                if (Timer_LGIT_LOT_FAIL.On(1000))
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        DateTime Timestamp_10711 = DateTime.Now;
        JTimer Timer_10711 = new JTimer();
        private FCResultType npFlowChart28_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (SysPara.EnableMes)
            {
                List<string> _Pocket_List = new List<string>();
                List<string> _Module_List = new List<string>();
                List<string> _LotID_List = new List<string>();
                List<string> _Cover_List = new List<string>();

                List<string> Tamp_LotID_List = new List<string>() { "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", };
                List<string> Tamp_PocketID_List = new List<string>() { "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", };
                List<string> Tamp_ModuleID_List = new List<string>() { "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", };
                List<string> Tamp_Cover_List = new List<string>() { "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", };

                var keys = MESLib.CommParas.LOT_START_VALUE_LIST.Keys.ToList();
                for (int i = 0; i < keys.Count; i++)
                {
                    int key = keys[i];
                    JObject jobj = MESLib.CommParas.LOT_START_VALUE_LIST[key];

                    string lotid = jobj["LOTID"].Value<string>();
                    string pocketid = jobj["POCKETID"].Value<string>();
                    string moduleid = jobj["MODULEID"].Value<string>();
                    string materialid = MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MATERIALs[key];

                    int index = key;

                    SysPara.PalletProductCount++;// record the pallect products

                    Tamp_LotID_List[index] = lotid;
                    Tamp_PocketID_List[index] = pocketid;
                    Tamp_ModuleID_List[index] = moduleid;
                    Tamp_Cover_List[index] = materialid;

                }
                for (int i = 0; i < Tamp_PocketID_List.Count; i++)
                {
                    if (Tamp_PocketID_List[i] != "")
                    {
                        _LotID_List.Add(Tamp_LotID_List[i]);
                        _Pocket_List.Add(Tamp_PocketID_List[i]);
                        _Module_List.Add(Tamp_ModuleID_List[i]);
                        _Cover_List.Add(Tamp_Cover_List[i]);
                    }
                }

                MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo("10711");
                Dictionary<string, string> parameters = new Dictionary<string, string>();
                string filename = "";
                filename = DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMddHHmmss") + ".csv";
                string filepath = @"D:\GMES\APDLog";
                filepath = System.IO.Path.Combine(filepath, DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMdd"));

                // check file path
                if (!System.IO.Directory.Exists(filepath))
                {
                    System.IO.Directory.CreateDirectory(filepath);
                }
                string csv_title = "Pallet_BCR,Cover_BCR,C/T";
                using (System.IO.StreamWriter sw = new StreamWriter(System.IO.Path.Combine(filepath, filename), true))
                {
                    sw.WriteLine(csv_title);
                }

                // create parameters
                string Pallet_BCR = "";
                string Cover_BCR = "";
                string ct = "";
                string _ct = (processData.swCycleTime.ElapsedMilliseconds / 1000.0).ToString("#.0");

                for (int i = 0; i < MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.POCKETIDs.Length; i++)
                {
                    string pocketid = MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.POCKETIDs[i];


                    // check each pocket id

                    if (!_Pocket_List.Contains(pocketid))
                    {
                        Pallet_BCR += "";
                        Cover_BCR += "";
                        ct += "";
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        int index = _Pocket_List.IndexOf(pocketid);
                        string pallet_bcr = _Module_List[index];
                        string cover_bcr = _Cover_List[index];
                        Pallet_BCR += pallet_bcr;
                        Cover_BCR += cover_bcr;
                        ct += _ct;
                        // csv file parameters                
                        using (System.IO.StreamWriter sw = new StreamWriter(System.IO.Path.Combine(filepath, filename), true))
                        {
                            sw.WriteLine(string.Format("{0},{1},{2}",
                                pallet_bcr,
                                cover_bcr,
                                _ct));
                        }
                    }
                    if (i != MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.POCKETIDs.Length - 1)
                    {
                        Pallet_BCR += ",";
                        Cover_BCR += ",";
                        ct += ",";
                    }
                }
                parameters.Add("C/T", _ct);
                parameters.Add("Pallet_BCR", Pallet_BCR);
                parameters.Add("Cover_BCR", Cover_BCR);

                Timestamp_10711 = DateTime.Now;
                Timer_10711.Restart();


                MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_10711(_LotID_List, _Cover_List, _Pocket_List,
                    _Module_List, MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PRODID,
                    MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PROCID,
                    parameters, filename, filepath, "", "");
            }
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart27_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (MESLib.CommParas.EnableMes)
            {
                if (MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_ACK_WithTimeout("10711", Timestamp_10711,
                    Timer_10711, out int ACK, out string data, out bool timeout))
                {
                    if (ACK == 0)
                    {
                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70100");
                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    if (timeout)
                    {
                        // T3 Fail
                        MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_11001("2");
                        MESLib.CommParas.MesCenter.StopRunning();
                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70102");
                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }
                }
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart31_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            int posCount = SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count + CheckProduct;


            string[] pos = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonPosition, "Convery Pallet CamPosition");
            MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "Command：" + pos[2]
                + "Pos：" + string.Format("{0:d3}", posCount + 1)
                + "AppIndex：" + pos[4], SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
            if (Robot.WriteToEpson(pos[2], string.Format("{0:d3}", posCount + 1), pos[4], 30000))
            {
                RunTM.Restart();
                OB_RobotLight.On();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart32_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            int ret = Robot.ReadFromEpson();
            if (ret == 1) { MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot to cammer runner position ok", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog); return FCResultType.NEXT; }
            else if (ret == 0)
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonErrInfo, Robot.ErorrCode)[1], SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            else if (RunTM.On(SysPara.TimeOutSec)) { MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot to cammer runner position timeout", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog); SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1635"); return FCResultType.IDLE; }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart33_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            bool IsLeftHaveProduct = true;

            try

                    IsRightHaveProduct = Convert.ToBoolean(H1_CheckProduct.TB.Outputs["Result_Right"].Value);

                    #region save img

                    //}

                    //}
                    #endregion




                    bCheckProduct[CheckProduct * 2] = IsLeftHaveProduct;
                    bCheckProduct[CheckProduct * 2 + 1] = IsRightHaveProduct;

                    if (!IsLeftHaveProduct || !IsRightHaveProduct)
                    {
                        MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "NG PCB", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                        string path1 = @"D:\WorkLog\ImgSave\CheckProduct\" + DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMdd") + "\\NGImage\\";
                        string imgname = DateTime.Now.ToString("HH_mm_ss_fff") + "_" + ".bmp";

                            if (temp)

                                bProductAllNG = false;
                                break;
                            }


                        }

                CCDAlogrithmStatus = false;
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1205");        //CCD fail

            }
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart29_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            //SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Clear();
            CheckProduct++;
            if (CheckProduct >= 6)
            {
                CheckProduct = 0;
                Product_Pallet_Code temp = new Product_Pallet_Code();
                if (bProductAllNG)
                {
                    for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++)
                    {
                        traycammerposition++;
                        temp.PressureResult = false;
                        temp.PalletCode = SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Dequeue();
                        temp.ProdcutCode = "";
                        temp.productcammerresult = "NG";
                        temp.productscanresult = "NG";
                        SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Enqueue(temp);
                    }
                    if (SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count >= 12)
                    {
                        OB_RobotLight.Off();

                        return FCResultType.CASE2;
                    }
                    return FCResultType.CASE1;
                }

                OB_RobotLight.Off();

                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }

            return FCResultType.CASE1;
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart18_FlowRun_1(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {


            try
            {
                string saveImgPath = MiddleLayer.GantryF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "SaveImagePath") + "\\";

                for (int i = 0; i < FeederImages.Count; i++)
                {
                    string imageName = string.Format(@"{0}_{1}_NA_NA_{2}_{3}.bmp",
                                        PCBCode[i],
                                         scanPellatCode[SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count + i],
                                          FeederImages[i].FeederTimer,
                                         MiddleLayer.MesF2.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EQPID")
                                            );
                    PCBCodePallet[SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Count + i] = PCBCode[i];
                    using (CogImageFileBMP img1 = new CogImageFileBMP())
                    {
                        if (!Directory.Exists(saveImgPath)) { Directory.CreateDirectory(saveImgPath); }
                        Image newimg = FeederImages[i].FeederImage;
                        newimg.Save(saveImgPath + imageName, System.Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat.Bmp);
                        newimg.Dispose();
                    }
                }
                FeederImages.Clear();
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {


            }
            try
            {
                string saveImgPath = MiddleLayer.GantryF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "SaveImagePath") + "\\";

                for (int i = 0; i < PalletImg.Count; i++)
                {
                    string imageName = string.Format(@"{0}_{1}_NA_NA_{2}_{3}.bmp",
                                        PCBCode[i],
                                         scanPellatCode[traycammerposition - 3 + i],
                                          PalletImg[i].FeederTimer,
                                         MiddleLayer.MesF2.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EQPID")
                                            );

                    using (CogImageFileBMP img1 = new CogImageFileBMP())
                    {
                        if (!Directory.Exists(saveImgPath)) { Directory.CreateDirectory(saveImgPath); }
                        Image newimg = PalletImg[i].FeederImage;
                        newimg.Save(saveImgPath + imageName, System.Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat.Bmp);
                        newimg.Dispose();
                    }
                }
                PalletImg.Clear();
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {


            }










            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private void button45_Click_1(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            DataTable dt = RecipeData.Tables["RSet"];
            dt.Rows[0]["PalletMinValueX"] = (double)num_minPX.Value;
            dt.Rows[0]["PalletMinValueY"] = (double)num_minPY.Value;
            dt.Rows[0]["PalletMaxValueX"] = (double)num_maxPX.Value;
            dt.Rows[0]["PalletMaxValueY"] = (double)num_maxPY.Value;
            dt.Rows[0]["AngleLimit"] = (double)num_anglelimit.Value;
            dt.AcceptChanges();
            this.WriteRecipeData(string.Format("{0}\\{1}.xml", SysPara.RecipeDataDirectory, SysPara.RecipeName));

        }

        //Aaron scanning test prototype
        private void btnStartTest_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            MiddleLayer.GantryF.OB_RobotPause.Off();
            MiddleLayer.GantryF.OB_RobotContinue.On();
            Thread.Sleep(500);
            MiddleLayer.GantryF.OB_RobotContinue.Off();
            this.npFlowChart34.TaskReset();
            scanningTestFlow = true;
            System.Threading.Tasks.Task.Factory.StartNew(() =>
            {
                while (scanningTestFlow)
                {
                    npFlowChart34.TaskRun();
                    Thread.Sleep(5);
                }
            });
        }

        }

        //Aaron scanning test prototype
        private FCResultType npFlowChart37_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            int ret = Robot.ReadFromEpson();
            if (ret == 1)
            {
                RunTM.Restart();
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot to scan product position ok", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else if (ret == 0)
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonErrInfo, Robot.ErorrCode)[1], SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            else if (RunTM.On(SysPara.TimeOutSec))
            { MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot to scan product position timeout", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog); SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1635"); return FCResultType.IDLE; }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart39_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (scanningTestIndex == 48)
            {
                scanningTestIndex = 0;
                string[] pos = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonPosition, "Goto Safety");
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot go safety", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                if (Robot.WriteToEpson(pos[2], pos[3], pos[4], 30000))
                {
                    InitTM.Restart();
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
                else
                {
                    return FCResultType.IDLE;
                }
            }
            else
            {
                return FCResultType.CASE1;
            }
        }
        //Aaron scanning test prototype
        private FCResultType npFlowChart40_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            int ret = Robot.ReadFromEpson();
            if (ret == 1)
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot to safety ok", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                scanningTestFlow = false;
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else if (ret == 0)
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonErrInfo, Robot.ErorrCode)[1] + "," + GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonErrInfo, Robot.ErorrCode)[3], SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            else if (InitTM.On(10000))
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot to safety timeout", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1635"); return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart41_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            GC.Collect();
            string processName = "FilesMonitor"; // 不包括 .exe
            System.Diagnostics.Process[] processes = System.Diagnostics.Process.GetProcessesByName(processName);

            if (processes.Length > 0)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"Program: {processName} existed");
            }
            else
            {
                string exePath = @"D:\CurProgram\FilesMonitor\FilesMonitor.exe";
                string workingDirectory = Path.GetDirectoryName(exePath);

                try
                {
                    System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo startInfo = new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
                    {
                        FileName = exePath,
                        WorkingDirectory = workingDirectory,
                        UseShellExecute = false
                    };

                    System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(startInfo);
                    Console.WriteLine($"Program: {processName} started");
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    Console.WriteLine($"Start Program Error: {ex.Message}");
                }
            }
            return FCResultType.NEXT;

        }

        private FCResultType RRP_0001_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)

            throwingjudge = false;
            traycammerposition = 0;
            needNGThrowingMaterial = false;
            NGGripperJudge = 0;
            curR2HaveNum1 = 0;
            //  SysPara.NGpositionCount = 0;
            JudgeGripperuseful = 0;
            Gripperuseful = "ok";
            needThrowing = 0;
            needThrowingcode = "ok";
            bInitialOk = false;
            isServoAlm = false;
            CCDAlogrithmStatus = false;
            isPickProduceOK = true;
            curPalletCode = "";
            curR1HaveNum = 0;
            curR2HaveNum1 = 0;
            SysPara.RIniRet = false;
            SysPara.JAGStarytEvent.Reset();
            SysPara.OverPressure = false;
            SysPara.Product_Result_XYU.Clear();
            CCDReuslt_EachLoop.Clear();//LFF-0209
            SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.Clear();
            SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Clear();
            SysPara.Que_Product_pallet_Code.Clear();
            SysPara.R1GrabCompeletStatus = false;            //R1 Grab feeder product compelet flag
            SysPara.ScanCount = 0;                            //feeder scan count
            SysPara.R1ScanResult = false;                    //scan state
            MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.NotifyAssembly = false;
            //SysPara.R1AssembStatus = false;                  //pallet call robot can assemb
            SysPara.ProductCount = 12;                        //assemb all count
            SysPara.FeederProductCount = 48;                        //feeder product count
            SysPara.R1AssembResult = false;                  //assemb result
            //SysPara.R1AssembCompeletStatus = false;      //R1 Assemb compelet flag
            MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.AssembCompeletStatus = false;
            SysPara.PressureCompelet = false;
            curProductCount = 0;
            scanCount = 0;
            haveSendCodeToMES = false;
            //SysPara.pallettransferout = true;
            #endregion
            SysPara.NPShowAlarm("8015");
            interactionBox = new ResetProcessSequence.Windows.InteractionBox("EL ROBOT SE MOVERA A LA POSICION SEGURA","FAVOR DE CONFIRMAR", true, true);
            interactionBox.ShowInteractionBox();
            if (interactionBox.dResult == DialogResult.OK)
            {
                InitTM.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }

        }

        private FCResultType flowChart86_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            SysPara.visionPos = new VisionPos();
            SysPara.visionposition = new VisionPosition();
            OB_RobotLight.On();
            DelayMs(300);
            try
            {
                if (H1_DownCCDPickup.TakePicture())         //CCD take picture
                {
                    H1_DownCCDPickup.RunTB();
                    RefreshDifferentThreadUI(cogRecDisp_H5_Recipe, () =>
                    {
                        cogRecDisp_H5_Recipe.StaticGraphics.Clear();
                        cogRecDisp_H5_Recipe.Image = H1_DownCCDPickup.InputImage;
                        cogRecDisp_H5_Recipe.Record = H1_DownCCDPickup.TB.CreateLastRunRecord().SubRecords[0];
                        cogRecDisp_H5_Recipe.Fit();
                    });
                    #region save img
                    try
                    {
                        string isAccept = H1_DownCCDPickup.IsAccept ? "OK\\" : "NG\\";

                        if (MiddleLayer.GantryF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EnableSaveOriginalImg"))
                        {
                            string path1 = @"D:\ImgSave\NGtray1Img\Original\" + DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMdd") + "\\" + isAccept;
                            string imgname = DateTime.Now.ToString("HH_mm_ss_fff") + "_" + SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.ToList()[curR2HaveNum1].Code + ".bmp";
                            using (CogImageFileBMP img = new CogImageFileBMP())
                            {
                                if (!Directory.Exists(path1)) { Directory.CreateDirectory(path1); }
                                img.Open(path1 + imgname, CogImageFileModeConstants.Write);
                                img.Append(H1_DownCCDPickup.InputImage);
                                img.Close();
                            }
                        }

                        if (MiddleLayer.GantryF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EnableSaveJPEGImg"))
                        {
                            string path2 = @"D:\ImgSave\NGtray1Img\Conversion\" + DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMdd") + "\\" + isAccept;
                            string imgname1 = DateTime.Now.ToString("HH_mm_ss_fff") + "_" + SysPara.Que_ProductCode_PickPos.ToList()[curR2HaveNum1].Code + ".bmp";
                            using (CogImageFileBMP img1 = new CogImageFileBMP())
                            {
                                if (!Directory.Exists(path2)) { Directory.CreateDirectory(path2); }
                                Image newimg = cogRecDisp_H5_Recipe.CreateContentBitmap(CogDisplayContentBitmapConstants.Display);
                                newimg.Save(path2 + imgname1, System.Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat.Jpeg);
                                newimg.Dispose();
                            }
                        }

                    }
                    catch (Exception)
                    {

                    }
                    #endregion
                    //if (!MiddleLayer.SystemF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "DisableVision"))
                    //{

                    if (Convert.ToBoolean(H1_DownCCDPickup.TB.Outputs["IsHaveProduct"].Value) == false)
                    {
                        MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot NG throwing position1 not have product", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                        OB_RobotLight.Off();
                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        string NGbufferIndex = "NGbuffer1";
                        switch (SysPara.NGpositionCount)
                        {
                            case 1:
                                NGbufferIndex = "NGbuffer1";
                                break;
                            case 2:
                                NGbufferIndex = "NGbuffer2";
                                break;
                            case 3:
                                NGbufferIndex = "NGbuffer3";
                                break;
                            default:
                                break;
                        }
                        DataTable dt = RecipeData.Tables["RSet"];
                        dt.Rows[0][NGbufferIndex] = true;
                        dt.AcceptChanges();
                        this.WriteRecipeData(string.Format("{0}\\{1}.xml", SysPara.RecipeDataDirectory, SysPara.RecipeName));
                        OB_RobotLight.Off();
                        MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot NG throwing position1 have product!!!need clear", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                        //SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1208");
                        return FCResultType.CASE1;
                    }
                    //}
                    //else
                    //{
                    //    SysPara.visionposition.X = "000.000";
                    //    SysPara.visionposition.Y = "000.000";
                    //    SysPara.visionposition.U = "000.000";
                    //    CCDAlogrithmStatus = true;
                    //    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    //}

                }
                else
                {
                    CCDAlogrithmStatus = false;
                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1205");        //CCD fail
                    return FCResultType.CASE1;
                }
            }
            catch
            {
                CCDAlogrithmStatus = false;
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1205");        //CCD fail
                return FCResultType.CASE1;
            }
        }

            }

                return FCResultType.NEXT;

            else if (ret == -104)

                return FCResultType.IDLE;

            {
                //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "set robot home speed 30 timeout", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1634");
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }

                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }

        private FCResultType flowChart63_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonErrInfo, Robot.ErorrCode)[1] == "E9000")
            {
                //SysPara.NPShowAlarm("0007");
                initialthrowgripper = 1;
                if (!GetRecipeValue("RSet", "NGbuffer1"))
                {
                    SysPara.NGpositionCount = 1;
                    initialthrowgripper = 1;
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
                else if (!GetRecipeValue("RSet", "NGbuffer2"))
                {
                    SysPara.NGpositionCount = 2;
                    initialthrowgripper = 1;
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
                else if (!GetRecipeValue("RSet", "NGbuffer3"))
                {
                    SysPara.NGpositionCount = 3;
                    initialthrowgripper = 1;
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
                else
                {
                    MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "NG had full", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("0006");
                    reintento.fnSetTextMessageNShow("NG tray is full!!! sure has been replaced?", true, false, true);
                    if (reintento.dResult == DialogResult.Yes)
                    {
                        DataTable dt = RecipeData.Tables["RSet"];
                        dt.Rows[0]["NGbuffer1"] = false;
                        dt.Rows[0]["NGbuffer2"] = false;
                        dt.Rows[0]["NGbuffer3"] = false;
                        dt.AcceptChanges();
                        SDKKernal.ClearAlarm("0006");
                        this.WriteRecipeData(string.Format("{0}\\{1}.xml", SysPara.RecipeDataDirectory, SysPara.RecipeName));
                        SysPara.NGpositionCount = 1;
                        NGfullcheck = true;
                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        DelayMs(300);
                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }
                }
            }
            else if (GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonErrInfo, Robot.ErorrCode)[1] == "E9001")
            {
                //SysPara.NPShowAlarm("0008");
                initialthrowgripper = 2;
                if (!GetRecipeValue("RSet", "NGbuffer1"))
                {
                    SysPara.NGpositionCount = 1;
                    initialthrowgripper = 2;
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
                else if (!GetRecipeValue("RSet", "NGbuffer2"))
                {
                    SysPara.NGpositionCount = 2;
                    initialthrowgripper = 2;
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
                else if (!GetRecipeValue("RSet", "NGbuffer3"))
                {
                    SysPara.NGpositionCount = 3;
                    initialthrowgripper = 2;
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
                else
                {
                    MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "NG had full", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("0006");
                    reintento.fnSetTextMessageNShow("NG tray is full!!! sure has been replaced?", true, false, true);
                    if (reintento.dResult == DialogResult.Yes)
                    {
                        DataTable dt = RecipeData.Tables["RSet"];
                        dt.Rows[0]["NGbuffer1"] = false;
                        dt.Rows[0]["NGbuffer2"] = false;
                        dt.Rows[0]["NGbuffer3"] = false;
                        dt.AcceptChanges();
                        SDKKernal.ClearAlarm("0006");
                        this.WriteRecipeData(string.Format("{0}\\{1}.xml", SysPara.RecipeDataDirectory, SysPara.RecipeName));
                        SysPara.NGpositionCount = 1;
                        NGfullcheck = true;
                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        DelayMs(300);
                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }
                }
            }
            else if (GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonErrInfo, Robot.ErorrCode)[1] == "E9002")
            {
                //SysPara.NPShowAlarm("0009");
                initialthrowgripper = 3;
                if (!GetRecipeValue("RSet", "NGbuffer1"))
                {
                    SysPara.NGpositionCount = 1;
                    initialthrowgripper = 3;
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
                else if (!GetRecipeValue("RSet", "NGbuffer2"))
                {
                    SysPara.NGpositionCount = 2;
                    initialthrowgripper = 3;
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
                else if (!GetRecipeValue("RSet", "NGbuffer3"))
                {
                    SysPara.NGpositionCount = 3;
                    initialthrowgripper = 3;
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
                else
                {
                    MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "NG had full", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("0006");
                    reintento.fnSetTextMessageNShow("NG tray is full!!! sure has been replaced?", true, false, true);
                    if (reintento.dResult == DialogResult.Yes)
                    {
                        DataTable dt = RecipeData.Tables["RSet"];
                        dt.Rows[0]["NGbuffer1"] = false;
                        dt.Rows[0]["NGbuffer2"] = false;
                        dt.Rows[0]["NGbuffer3"] = false;
                        dt.AcceptChanges();
                        SDKKernal.ClearAlarm("0006");
                        this.WriteRecipeData(string.Format("{0}\\{1}.xml", SysPara.RecipeDataDirectory, SysPara.RecipeName));
                        SysPara.NGpositionCount = 1;
                        NGfullcheck = true;
                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        DelayMs(500);
                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }
                }
            }
            else
            {
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1635");
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
        }

            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }
        private FCResultType flowChart65_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            //if(SysPara.NGpositionCount==1&& initialthrowgripper==3)
            //{
            //    if (!GetRecipeValue("RSet", "NGbuffer2"))
            //    {
            //        SysPara.NGpositionCount = 2;
            //    }
            //    else if (!GetRecipeValue("RSet", "NGbuffer3"))
            //    {
            //        SysPara.NGpositionCount = 3;
            //    }
            //    else
            //    {
            //        DataTable dt = RecipeData.Tables["RSet"];
            //        dt.Rows[0]["NGbuffer1"] = true;
            //        dt.AcceptChanges();
            //        this.WriteRecipeData(string.Format("{0}\\{1}.xml", SysPara.RecipeDataDirectory, SysPara.RecipeName));
            //        return FCResultType.CASE1;
            //    }
            //}

            if (Robot.WriteToEpson("016", string.Format("{0:d3}", SysPara.NGpositionCount), string.Format("{0:d2}", initialthrowgripper), 20000))
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot to throwing position", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                RunTM.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart66_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            int ret = Robot.ReadFromEpson();
            if (ret == 1) { MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot to throwing position ok", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog); return FCResultType.NEXT; }
            else if (ret == 0)
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonErrInfo, Robot.ErorrCode)[2], SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            else if (RunTM.On(SysPara.TimeOutSec)) { MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "robot to throwing position timeout", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog); SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1634"); return FCResultType.IDLE; }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart67_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            string NGbufferIndex = "NGbuffer1";
            switch (SysPara.NGpositionCount)
            {
                case 1:
                    NGbufferIndex = "NGbuffer1";
                    break;
                case 2:
                    NGbufferIndex = "NGbuffer2";
                    break;
                case 3:
                    NGbufferIndex = "NGbuffer3";
                    break;
            }
            DataTable dt = RecipeData.Tables["RSet"];
            dt.Rows[0][NGbufferIndex] = true;
            dt.AcceptChanges();
            this.WriteRecipeData(string.Format("{0}\\{1}.xml", SysPara.RecipeDataDirectory, SysPara.RecipeName));
            //if (Convert.ToBoolean(dt.Rows[0]["NGbuffer1"]) == true
            //    && Convert.ToBoolean(dt.Rows[0]["NGbuffer2"]) == true
            //    && Convert.ToBoolean(dt.Rows[0]["NGbuffer3"]) == true)
            //{
            //    MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogForm.LogType.Production, "ng had full", SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
            //    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("12005");
            //}
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private void chk_Output_15_CheckedChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (chk_Output_15.Checked)
            {
                chk_Output_15.BackgroundImage = Properties.Resources.Green;
                WriteIO[14] = "1";
            }
            else
            {
                chk_Output_15.BackgroundImage = Properties.Resources.Red;
                WriteIO[14] = "0";
            }
        }

        private void chk_Output_16_CheckedChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (chk_Output_16.Checked)
            {
                chk_Output_16.BackgroundImage = Properties.Resources.Green;
                WriteIO[15] = "1";
            }
            else
            {
                chk_Output_16.BackgroundImage = Properties.Resources.Red;
                WriteIO[15] = "0";
            }
        }
        public bool GetRobotDO()
        {
            string[] pos1 = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_EpsonPosition, "Write RobotD0");
            string[] s1 = new string[7];
            string[] s2 = new string[7];
            string[] s3 = new string[7];
            for (int i = 0; i < 21; i++)
            {
                if (i < 7) { s1[i] = WriteIO[i]; }
                else if (i >= 7 && i < 14) { s2[i - 7] = WriteIO[i]; }
                else if (i >= 14 && i < 16) { s3[i - 14] = WriteIO[i]; }
                else { s3[i - 14] = "F"; }
            }
            bool ret = Robot.WriteToEpson(pos1[2], pos1[3], "FF", 30000, string.Join("", s1), string.Join("", s2), string.Join("", s3));
            return ret;
        }
        private void tabControl4_Selected(object sender, TabControlEventArgs e)
        {
            if (tabControl2.SelectedIndex == 2)
            {
                if (Robot.ConnectStatus)
                {
                    bt_ReadIO_Click(null, null);
                }
            }
        }
        #endregion

        // 1->3,3->1
        public int InvertOrder(int Num)
        {
            switch (curProductCount)
            {
                case 3:
                    return Num * (-1) + 4;
                case 2:
                    return Num * (-1) + 3;
                case 1:
                    return Num * (-1) + 2;
                default:
                    return Num * (-1) + 4;
            }
        }

        public int InvertOrder1(int Num)
        {
            return Num * (-1) + 3;
        }

        public int GetRAppIndex(int num)
        {
            switch (num % 3)
            {
                case 0:
                    return 1;
                case 1:
                    return 2;
                case 2:
                    return 3;
                default:
                    return 0;
            }
        }

                return true;
            }
            else { return false; }
        }

        private void tabPage19_Enter(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            tm_ReadFFUSpeed.Start();
        }

        private void tabPage19_Leave(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            tm_ReadFFUSpeed.Stop();
        }

        private void tm_ReadFFUSpeed_Tick(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EnableByPass"))
            {
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("0008");


            }
            else
            {
                SDKKernal.ClearAlarm("0008");
            }
            if (MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "IsDryCycleMode"))
            {
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("0011");


            }
            else
            {
                SDKKernal.ClearAlarm("0011");
            }

            if (MiddleLayer.SystemF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "DisableFeeder"))
            {
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("0013");


            }
            else
            {
                SDKKernal.ClearAlarm("0013");
            }

            if (MiddleLayer.SystemF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "DisableVision"))
            {
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("0014");


            }
            else
            {
                SDKKernal.ClearAlarm("0014");
            }


        }

        public void SaveAxisPosPara()
        {
            string epsonPositionPath = Path.Combine(Application.StartupPath, @"ModuleData\EpsonPosition.xml");
            dgv_EpsonPosition.SaveXmlFileFromPath(epsonPositionPath);
            string epsonErrInfoPath = Path.Combine(Application.StartupPath, @"ModuleData\EpsonErrInfo.xml");
            dgv_EpsonErrInfo.SaveXmlFileFromPath(epsonErrInfoPath);
            string RobotPositionInfoPath = Path.Combine(Application.StartupPath, @"ModuleData\RobotTeachPosInfo.xml");
            dgv_RobotPosition.SaveXmlFileFromPath(RobotPositionInfoPath);
        }
        //Load all tables position()
        public void ReadAxisPara()
        {
            string epsonPositionPath = Path.Combine(Application.StartupPath, @"ModuleData\EpsonPosition.xml");
            dgv_EpsonPosition.LoadXmlFileFromPath(epsonPositionPath);
            string epsonErrInfoPath = Path.Combine(Application.StartupPath, @"ModuleData\EpsonErrInfo.xml");
            dgv_EpsonErrInfo.LoadXmlFileFromPath(epsonErrInfoPath);
            string RobotPositionInfoPath = Path.Combine(Application.StartupPath, @"ModuleData\RobotTeachPosInfo.xml");
            dgv_RobotPosition.LoadXmlFileFromPath(RobotPositionInfoPath);

        }
